2. 역할 설명이 불가능한 파일은 **정리 후보**로 분류한다.

4. 판단 우선순위는 다음과 같다:

# File name: model_service.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# services/model_service.py - GPU Inference Service with Concurrency Control
"""
Model Service for Single-GPU Environment

This module provides thread-safe GPU inference with asyncio.Lock to prevent
OOM errors and race conditions when multiple requests arrive simultaneously.

Key Features:
- asyncio.Lock ensures only one inference runs at a time
- Other requests queue and wait without blocking the event loop
- Supports both streaming and non-streaming inference
"""
import asyncio
import logging
import os
from typing import Optional, Generator, AsyncGenerator, Callable, List, Dict, Any
from functools import lru_cache

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# =============================================================================
# Global GPU Lock - Ensures single inference at a time
# =============================================================================
gpu_inference_lock = asyncio.Lock()

# Queue status tracking (for monitoring)
_queue_status = {
    "waiting": 0,
    "processing": False,
    "total_processed": 0
}


def get_queue_status() -> dict:
    """Get current inference queue status."""
    return _queue_status.copy()


# =============================================================================
# Model Loading (Singleton Pattern)
# =============================================================================

# Model paths
MAIN_MODEL_PATH = "models/Qwen3-14B-Q4_K_M.gguf"
FAST_MODEL_PATH = "models/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf"

# Model instances (lazy loaded)
_main_model = None
_fast_model = None
_models_loaded = False


def _load_models():
    """Load models lazily on first use."""
    global _main_model, _fast_model, _models_loaded

    if _models_loaded:
        return

    try:
        from llama_cpp import Llama
        LLAMA_CPP_AVAILABLE = True
    except ImportError:
        LLAMA_CPP_AVAILABLE = False
        logger.warning("llama-cpp-python not available")
        _models_loaded = True
        return

    # Load Main Model (Qwen 14B)
    if os.path.exists(MAIN_MODEL_PATH):
        logger.info(f"Loading Main Model: {MAIN_MODEL_PATH}")
        try:
            _main_model = Llama(
                model_path=MAIN_MODEL_PATH,
                n_ctx=16384,
                n_gpu_layers=-1,
                verbose=False
            )
            logger.info("Main Model (Qwen 14B) loaded successfully")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load Main Model: {e}")
    else:
        logger.warning(f"Main Model not found: {MAIN_MODEL_PATH}")

    # Load Fast Model (Qwen 3B)
    if os.path.exists(FAST_MODEL_PATH):
        logger.info(f"Loading Fast Model: {FAST_MODEL_PATH}")
        try:
            _fast_model = Llama(
                model_path=FAST_MODEL_PATH,
                n_ctx=4096,
                n_gpu_layers=-1,
                verbose=False
            )
            logger.info("Fast Model (Qwen 3B) loaded successfully")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load Fast Model: {e}")
    else:
        logger.warning(f"Fast Model not found: {FAST_MODEL_PATH}")

    _models_loaded = True


def get_main_model():
    """Get the main model instance."""
    _load_models()
    return _main_model


def get_fast_model():
    """Get the fast model instance."""
    _load_models()
    return _fast_model


# =============================================================================
# Model Service Class
# =============================================================================

class ModelService:
    """
    GPU Inference Service with Concurrency Control.

    Uses asyncio.Lock to ensure only one inference runs at a time,
    preventing OOM errors on single-GPU setups.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.lock = gpu_inference_lock
        self._load_models()

    def _load_models(self):
        """Ensure models are loaded."""
        _load_models()

    @property
    def main_model(self):
        return get_main_model()

    @property
    def fast_model(self):
        return get_fast_model()

    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        """Check if any model is available."""
        return self.main_model is not None or self.fast_model is not None

    def get_status(self) -> dict:
        """Get service status."""
        return {
            "main_model_loaded": self.main_model is not None,
            "fast_model_loaded": self.fast_model is not None,
            "lock_locked": self.lock.locked(),
            "queue_status": get_queue_status()
        }

    def _select_model(self, mode: str, question_text: str = ""):
        """Select appropriate model based on mode."""
        if mode == "fast":
            return self.fast_model, "Fast (Qwen 3B)", 2048
        elif mode == "auto":
            # Auto mode: select based on question complexity
            if len(question_text) < 15:
                return self.fast_model, "Fast (Qwen 3B)", 2048
            else:
                return self.main_model, "Main (Qwen 14B)", 8192
        else:  # thinking, research
            return self.main_model, "Main (Qwen 14B)", 8192

    def _build_prompt(self, system_prompt: str, user_prompt: str) -> str:
        """Build ChatML format prompt."""
        return f"<|im_start|>system\n{system_prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>user\n{user_prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"

    # =========================================================================
    # Synchronous Inference (with lock)
    # =========================================================================

    def generate_sync(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        mode: str = "thinking",
        question_text: str = ""
    ) -> str:
        """
        Synchronous generation - blocks until inference complete.
        NOTE: This should only be called from within an async context
        that has already acquired the lock.
        """
        model, model_name, max_tokens = self._select_model(mode, question_text)

        if not model:
            return f"Model not available: {model_name}"

        prompt = self._build_prompt(system_prompt, user_prompt)

        try:
            logger.info(f"[GPU Inference] Using {model_name}, mode={mode}")
            output = model(
                prompt,
                max_tokens=max_tokens,
                stop=["<|im_end|>"],
                echo=False,
                temperature=0.7
            )
            return output['choices'][0]['text'].strip()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Generation error ({model_name}): {e}")
            return f"Generation error: {str(e)}"

    def generate_stream_sync(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        mode: str = "thinking",
        question_text: str = ""
    ) -> Generator[str, None, None]:
        """
        Synchronous streaming generation.
        NOTE: This should only be called from within an async context
        that has already acquired the lock.
        """
        model, model_name, max_tokens = self._select_model(mode, question_text)

        if not model:
            yield f"Model not available: {model_name}"
            return

        prompt = self._build_prompt(system_prompt, user_prompt)

        try:
            logger.info(f"[GPU Inference - STREAM] Using {model_name}, mode={mode}")
            stream = model(
                prompt,
                max_tokens=max_tokens,
                stop=["<|im_end|>"],
                echo=False,
                temperature=0.7,
                stream=True
            )

            for chunk in stream:
                if 'choices' in chunk and len(chunk['choices']) > 0:
                    delta = chunk['choices'][0].get('text', '')
                    if delta:
                        yield delta

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Streaming error ({model_name}): {e}")
            yield f"Streaming error: {str(e)}"

    # =========================================================================
    # Async Inference Wrappers (with lock acquisition)
    # =========================================================================

    async def generate(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        mode: str = "thinking",
        question_text: str = ""
    ) -> str:
        """
        Async generation with automatic lock acquisition.

        This method:
        1. Acquires the GPU lock (waits if another inference is running)
        2. Runs the inference
        3. Releases the lock

        Other requests will queue and wait without blocking the event loop.
        """
        global _queue_status
        _queue_status["waiting"] += 1

        logger.info(f"[Queue] Waiting for GPU lock... (queue size: {_queue_status['waiting']})")

        async with self.lock:
            _queue_status["waiting"] -= 1
            _queue_status["processing"] = True

            try:
                logger.info("[Queue] Lock acquired, starting inference...")
                # Run sync inference in thread pool to not block event loop
                result = await asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(
                    None,
                    lambda: self.generate_sync(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode, question_text)
                )
                _queue_status["total_processed"] += 1
                return result
            finally:
                _queue_status["processing"] = False
                logger.info("[Queue] Lock released")

    async def generate_stream(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        mode: str = "thinking",
        question_text: str = ""
    ) -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:
        """
        Async streaming generation with automatic lock acquisition.

        Yields text chunks in real-time while holding the GPU lock.
        The lock is held for the entire duration of streaming.

        Usage:
            async for chunk in service.generate_stream(...):
                yield chunk
        """
        global _queue_status
        _queue_status["waiting"] += 1

        logger.info(f"[Queue - Stream] Waiting for GPU lock... (queue size: {_queue_status['waiting']})")

        async with self.lock:
            _queue_status["waiting"] -= 1
            _queue_status["processing"] = True

            try:
                logger.info("[Queue - Stream] Lock acquired, starting streaming inference...")

                # Create the sync generator
                sync_gen = self.generate_stream_sync(
                    system_prompt, user_prompt, mode, question_text
                )

                # Yield chunks from sync generator
                # We wrap this in run_in_executor to not block
                loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()

                def get_next_chunk(gen):
                    try:
                        return next(gen)
                    except StopIteration:
                        return None

                while True:
                    chunk = await loop.run_in_executor(None, get_next_chunk, sync_gen)
                    if chunk is None:
                        break
                    yield chunk

                _queue_status["total_processed"] += 1

            finally:
                _queue_status["processing"] = False
                logger.info("[Queue - Stream] Lock released")


# =============================================================================
# Module-level Service Instance (Singleton)
# =============================================================================

_model_service: Optional[ModelService] = None


def get_model_service() -> ModelService:
    """Get the singleton ModelService instance."""
    global _model_service
    if _model_service is None:
        _model_service = ModelService()
    return _model_service


# =============================================================================
# Convenience Functions (for backward compatibility)
# =============================================================================

async def locked_generate(
    system_prompt: str,
    user_prompt: str,
    mode: str = "thinking",
    question_text: str = ""
) -> str:
    """Convenience function for locked generation."""
    service = get_model_service()
    return await service.generate(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode, question_text)


async def locked_generate_stream(
    system_prompt: str,
    user_prompt: str,
    mode: str = "thinking",
    question_text: str = ""
) -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:
    """Convenience function for locked streaming generation."""
    service = get_model_service()
    async for chunk in service.generate_stream(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode, question_text):
        yield chunk

# File name: rag_ops.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# rag_ops.py — RAG Core Engine (Clean & Updated)
"""
RAG (검색 증강 생성) 시스템의 핵심 로직
- 지원 포맷: PDF, TXT, MD, CSV, Excel, Word, PowerPoint
- 문서 로드, 임베딩, 벡터 저장소 관리
"""

import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
import logging

# ==========================================
# [수정] 최신 LangChain 패키지 경로로 변경
# ==========================================
try:
    # 1. 문서 로더들
    from langchain_community.document_loaders import (
        PyPDFLoader, 
        TextLoader, 
        CSVLoader, 
        Docx2txtLoader, 
        UnstructuredExcelLoader, 
        UnstructuredPowerPointLoader
    )
    # 2. 텍스트 분할기 (여기가 노란줄 원인이었습니다!)
    from langchain_text_splitters import RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter
    
    # 3. 임베딩 & 벡터 DB
    from langchain_huggingface import HuggingFaceEmbeddings
    from langchain_chroma import Chroma
    
    LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError as e:
    print(f"⚠️ Import Error: {e}")
    # 혹시 langchain-text-splitters가 없다고 에러나면 설치 안내
    print("팁: 'pip install langchain-text-splitters'를 실행해보세요.")
    LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE = False

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
CHROMA_PATH = BASE_DIR / "chroma_db"
UPLOAD_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "uploads"

# 임베딩 모델
EMBEDDING_MODEL = "intfloat/multilingual-e5-large"

# 전역 임베딩 인스턴스
_embedding_instance = None


def get_embeddings():
    """임베딩 모델 싱글톤"""
    global _embedding_instance
    if _embedding_instance is None:
        if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE:
            raise RuntimeError("LangChain dependencies not installed")

        logger.info(f"🔄 Loading embedding model: {EMBEDDING_MODEL}")
        _embedding_instance = HuggingFaceEmbeddings(
            model_name=EMBEDDING_MODEL,
            model_kwargs={'device': 'cpu'}, 
            encode_kwargs={'normalize_embeddings': True}
        )
        logger.info("✅ Embedding model loaded")

    return _embedding_instance


# =========================
# Core Functions
# =========================

def process_and_index(file_path: str, collection_name: str) -> Dict:
    """다양한 파일을 로드하고 ChromaDB에 인덱싱"""
    if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE:
        return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": "LangChain not installed"}

    try:
        path_obj = Path(file_path)
        if not path_obj.exists():
            return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": f"File not found: {file_path}"}
        
        ext = path_obj.suffix.lower()
        loader = None

        # 로더 선택
        try:
            if ext == '.pdf':
                loader = PyPDFLoader(str(path_obj))
            elif ext == '.csv':
                loader = CSVLoader(str(path_obj), encoding='utf-8')
            elif ext in ['.xlsx', '.xls']:
                loader = UnstructuredExcelLoader(str(path_obj))
            elif ext in ['.docx', '.doc']:
                loader = Docx2txtLoader(str(path_obj))
            elif ext in ['.pptx', '.ppt']:
                loader = UnstructuredPowerPointLoader(str(path_obj))
            elif ext in ['.txt', '.md', '.json', '.html', '.py', '.log', '.yml', '.yaml']:
                loader = TextLoader(str(path_obj), encoding='utf-8')
            else:
                return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": f"Unsupported file extension: {ext}"}
            
            logger.info(f"📂 Loading file: {path_obj.name} ({ext})")
            documents = loader.load()
            
        except Exception as load_err:
            return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": f"Loader Error: {str(load_err)}"}

        if not documents:
            return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": "Document is empty"}

        logger.info(f"📄 Loaded {len(documents)} docs from {path_obj.name}")

        # 텍스트 분할
        text_splitter = RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter(
            chunk_size=1000,
            chunk_overlap=200,
            length_function=len,
            separators=["\n\n", "\n", ".", "!", "?", ",", " ", ""]
        )
        chunks = text_splitter.split_documents(documents)
        
        if not chunks:
            return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": "No chunks created"}

        # 메타데이터 추가
        for chunk in chunks:
            chunk.metadata['source_file'] = path_obj.name
            chunk.metadata['collection'] = collection_name

        # ChromaDB 저장
        embeddings = get_embeddings()
        vectorstore = Chroma(
            collection_name=collection_name,
            embedding_function=embeddings,
            persist_directory=str(CHROMA_PATH)
        )

        vectorstore.add_documents(chunks)
        logger.info(f"💾 Indexed {len(chunks)} chunks to '{collection_name}'")

        return {
            "success": True,
            "chunks": len(chunks),
            "message": f"Successfully indexed {len(chunks)} chunks"
        }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ Indexing error: {e}")
        return {"success": False, "chunks": 0, "message": str(e)}


def search_documents(query: str, collection_name: str, k: int = 3) -> List[Dict]:
    """유사도 검색 (강화된 디버깅)"""
    if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE:
        print(f"❌ [RAG SEARCH] LangChain not available")
        return []

    try:
        print(f"\n🔍 [RAG SEARCH] Starting search...")
        print(f"   - Query: {query[:100]}...")
        print(f"   - Collection: {collection_name}")
        print(f"   - K: {k}")

        embeddings = get_embeddings()
        vectorstore = Chroma(
            collection_name=collection_name,
            embedding_function=embeddings,
            persist_directory=str(CHROMA_PATH)
        )

        # 컬렉션 존재 확인
        try:
            collection = vectorstore._collection
            count = collection.count()
            print(f"   - Collection exists: {count} documents")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   - Collection check error: {e}")

        results = vectorstore.similarity_search_with_score(query, k=k)

        formatted_results = []
        for i, (doc, score) in enumerate(results, 1):
            formatted_results.append({
                "text": doc.page_content,
                "score": float(score),
                "metadata": doc.metadata
            })
            print(f"   - Result {i}: score={score:.4f}, length={len(doc.page_content)} chars")

        print(f"✅ [RAG SEARCH] Found {len(formatted_results)} results\n")
        return formatted_results

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ [RAG SEARCH] Error: {e}")
        print(f"❌ [RAG SEARCH] Exception: {e}")
        return []

def delete_collection(collection_name: str) -> bool:
    """컬렉션 삭제"""
    if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE: return False
    try:
        embeddings = get_embeddings()
        vectorstore = Chroma(
            collection_name=collection_name,
            embedding_function=embeddings,
            persist_directory=str(CHROMA_PATH)
        )
        vectorstore.delete_collection()
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ Delete error: {e}")
        return False

def get_collection_stats(collection_name: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
    """통계 조회"""
    if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE: return None
    try:
        embeddings = get_embeddings()
        vectorstore = Chroma(
            collection_name=collection_name,
            embedding_function=embeddings,
            persist_directory=str(CHROMA_PATH)
        )
        return {
            "name": collection_name,
            "count": vectorstore._collection.count()
        }
    except Exception:
        return None

def ensure_upload_dir(subdir: str = "") -> Path:
    """디렉토리 생성"""
    upload_path = UPLOAD_DIR / subdir if subdir else UPLOAD_DIR
    upload_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return upload_path

def get_all_collections() -> List[str]:
    """ChromaDB의 모든 컬렉션 이름 조회"""
    if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE:
        return []

    try:
        import chromadb
        client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=str(CHROMA_PATH))
        collections = client.list_collections()
        return [col.name for col in collections]
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ Failed to list collections: {e}")
        return []

def sync_db_with_files() -> Dict[str, int]:
    """
    파일-DB 동기화: 실제로 존재하지 않는 파일의 벡터 데이터를 ChromaDB에서 제거

    Returns:
        Dict with 'total_removed' count and collection-specific counts
    """
    if not LANGCHAIN_AVAILABLE:
        logger.warning("⚠️ LangChain not available, skipping DB sync")
        return {"total_removed": 0}

    try:
        import chromadb

        logger.info("🔄 [DB Sync] Starting file-database synchronization...")

        client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=str(CHROMA_PATH))
        collections = client.list_collections()

        total_removed = 0
        sync_report = {}

        for collection in collections:
            collection_name = collection.name
            logger.info(f"   📂 Checking collection: {collection_name}")

            try:
                # 컬렉션의 모든 문서 조회
                col = client.get_collection(name=collection_name)
                results = col.get(include=['metadatas'])

                if not results or not results.get('ids'):
                    logger.info(f"      └─ Empty collection, skipping")
                    continue

                ids_to_delete = []

                # 각 문서의 source_file 확인
                for doc_id, metadata in zip(results['ids'], results['metadatas']):
                    if not metadata:
                        continue

                    source_file = metadata.get('source_file', '')
                    if not source_file:
                        continue

                    # 파일 경로 확인 (여러 가능한 경로 시도)
                    file_exists = False

                    # 1. 절대 경로로 시도
                    if os.path.exists(source_file):
                        file_exists = True

                    # 2. uploads 폴더 내 경로로 시도
                    upload_path = UPLOAD_DIR / source_file
                    if upload_path.exists():
                        file_exists = True

                    # 3. temp 폴더 경로 시도
                    temp_path = UPLOAD_DIR / "temp" / source_file
                    if temp_path.exists():
                        file_exists = True

                    # 파일이 없으면 삭제 대상에 추가
                    if not file_exists:
                        ids_to_delete.append(doc_id)
                        logger.debug(f"      └─ Orphaned: {source_file} (ID: {doc_id})")

                # 삭제 실행
                if ids_to_delete:
                    col.delete(ids=ids_to_delete)
                    removed_count = len(ids_to_delete)
                    total_removed += removed_count
                    sync_report[collection_name] = removed_count
                    logger.info(f"      ✅ Removed {removed_count} orphaned documents from '{collection_name}'")
                else:
                    logger.info(f"      ✅ No orphaned documents in '{collection_name}'")
                    sync_report[collection_name] = 0

            except Exception as col_err:
                logger.error(f"      ❌ Error syncing collection '{collection_name}': {col_err}")
                sync_report[collection_name] = -1  # Error marker

        logger.info(f"✅ [DB Sync] Completed! Removed {total_removed} orphaned documents from ChromaDB")

        return {
            "total_removed": total_removed,
            "collections": sync_report
        }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ [DB Sync] Error during synchronization: {e}")
        return {"total_removed": 0, "error": str(e)}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    print("📦 RAG Engine Initialized")

const API_BASE = window.location.origin; //✅ [P0] API 기본 URL (state.js에서 참조)

// =========================
// AbortController Lifecycle
// =========================

/**
 * 새 AbortController 생성
 */
function createAbort() {
    abortController = new AbortController();
    return abortController;
}

/**
 * 현재 활성 요청 중단
 */
function abortActive() {
    if (abortController) {
        abortController.abort();
        abortController = null;
    }
}

/**
 * 중단 후 상태 초기화
 */
function stopGeneration() {
    abortActive();
    isGenerating = false;
    updateSendButtonState(false);
    document.getElementById('statusText').textContent = 'Stopped';
}

// =========================
// API Fetch Wrappers
// =========================

/**
 * Authorization 헤더 자동 추가 fetch
 */
async function apiFetch(path, options = {}) {
    const headers = { ...options.headers };
    if (AUTH_TOKEN) {
        headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`;
    }

    return fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
        ...options,
        headers
    });
}

/**
 * SSE 스트리밍 요청
 * @param {string} path - API 경로
 * @param {object} payload - 요청 body
 * @param {function} onDataLine - 데이터 라인 콜백 (line) => void
 * @param {AbortSignal} signal - abort signal
 */
async function apiStreamAsk(path, payload, onDataLine, signal) {
    const headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    };
    if (AUTH_TOKEN) {
        headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`;
    }

    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
        signal
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const reader = response.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let buffer = '';

    while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;

        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buffer.split('\n');
        buffer = lines.pop() || '';

        for (const line of lines) {
            if (line.trim()) {
                onDataLine(line);
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 모드 토글
 */
function toggleMode() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('modeToggle');
    const icon = document.getElementById('modeIcon');
    const text = document.getElementById('modeText');

    if (CURRENT_MODE === "auto") {
        CURRENT_MODE = "fast";
        icon.className = "fas fa-bolt"; text.textContent = "Fast";
        btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #f59e0b 0%, #f97316 100%)";
        btn.style.borderColor = "#f97316";
    } else if (CURRENT_MODE === "fast") {
        CURRENT_MODE = "thinking";
        icon.className = "fas fa-brain"; text.textContent = "Thinking";
        btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #6366f1 0%, #8b5cf6 100%)";
        btn.style.borderColor = "#8b5cf6";
    } else if (CURRENT_MODE === "thinking") {
        if (IS_GUEST_MODE) {
            CURRENT_MODE = "auto";
            icon.className = "fas fa-robot"; text.textContent = "Auto";
            btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #10b981 0%, #059669 100%)";
            btn.style.borderColor = "#059669";
            const st = document.getElementById('statusText');
            st.textContent = "⚠️ Research mode requires login";
            st.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
            setTimeout(() => { st.textContent = 'Ready'; st.classList.remove('text-yellow-400'); }, 2000);
        } else {
            CURRENT_MODE = "research";
            icon.className = "fas fa-microscope"; text.textContent = "Research";
            btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6 0%, #2563eb 100%)";
            btn.style.borderColor = "#2563eb";
        }
    } else {
        CURRENT_MODE = "auto";
        icon.className = "fas fa-robot"; text.textContent = "Auto";
        btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #10b981 0%, #059669 100%)";
        btn.style.borderColor = "#059669";
    }
}

/**
 * 파일 업로드 핸들러
 */
async function handleFileUpload(input) {
    const f=input.files[0]; if(!f) return;
    const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('file',f);
    document.getElementById('uploadStatusText').textContent='Uploading...'; document.getElementById('uploadPreview').classList.remove('hidden');
    const url = CURRENT_FOLDER_ID ? `${API_BASE}/folders/${CURRENT_FOLDER_ID}/upload` : `${API_BASE}/chat/upload/temp`;
    if(!CURRENT_FOLDER_ID && TEMP_SESSION_ID) fd.append('session_id', TEMP_SESSION_ID);
    const res = await fetch(url, {method:'POST',body:fd, headers:CURRENT_FOLDER_ID?{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}:{}});
    if(res.ok) { 
        const d=await res.json(); if(!CURRENT_FOLDER_ID) TEMP_SESSION_ID=d.session_id; 
        document.getElementById('uploadStatusText').textContent='Done!'; 
    } else document.getElementById('uploadStatusText').textContent='Failed';
}

/**
 * 리포트 모달
 */
function showReportModal() {
    document.getElementById('reportModal').style.display = 'flex';
    // 입력 필드 초기화
    document.getElementById('reportCategory').value = 'bug';
    document.getElementById('reportSummary').value = '';
    document.getElementById('reportDetails').value = '';
}


async function submitReport() {
    const category = document.getElementById('reportCategory').value;
    const summary = document.getElementById('reportSummary').value.trim();
    const details = document.getElementById('reportDetails').value.trim();

    // 입력 검증
    if (!summary) {
        alert('Please enter a summary');
        return;
    }

    if (!details) {
        alert('Please enter details');
        return;
    }

    try {
        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/chat/report`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                ...(AUTH_TOKEN ? {'Authorization': `Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`} : {})
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                category: category,
                summary: summary,
                details: details,
                session_id: CURRENT_SESSION_ID,
                message_id: null  // 필요시 마지막 메시지 ID 추가 가능
            })
        });

        if (response.ok) {
            const result = await response.json();
            alert('✅ Report submitted successfully! Thank you for your feedback.');
            closeModal('reportModal');
        } else {
            const error = await response.json();
            alert(`❌ Failed to submit report: ${error.detail || 'Unknown error'}`);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Report submission error:', error);
        alert('❌ Network error. Please try again.');
    }
}

/**
 * 앱 초기화
 */
window.onload = async () => {
    console.log('🚀 App loading...');

    // ============================================
    // [STEP 1] Access Gate Check (MUST PASS FIRST)
    // ============================================
    const hasAccess = await checkAccessGate();
    if (!hasAccess) {
        console.log('🔒 Access gate active - waiting for authentication');
        // Don't initialize rest of app until authenticated
        // The page will reload after successful login
        return;
    }

    // ============================================
    // [STEP 2] Normal App Initialization
    // ============================================
    document.getElementById('tempSlider').addEventListener('input', updateTempDisplay);

    // 인증 상태 확인 및 UI 업데이트
    if (AUTH_TOKEN) {
        await checkAuth();
    } else {
        switchToGuestUI();
    }

    // 온도 슬라이더 초기화
    updateTempDisplay();

    // ✅ [SESSION PERSISTENCE] URL 파라미터 자동 로드 (새로고침 유지)
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const sessionId = urlParams.get('session_id');

    if (sessionId) {
        console.log(`🔗 [URL] Found session_id=${sessionId}, auto-loading...`);

        // 세션 자동 로드 (약간의 지연으로 UI 초기화 완료 대기)
        setTimeout(async () => {
            try {
                await loadSession(parseInt(sessionId));
                console.log(`✅ [URL] Auto-loaded session ${sessionId}`);
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(`❌ [URL] Failed to auto-load session ${sessionId}:`, e);
                // 실패 시 URL 파라미터 제거
                history.replaceState(null, '', window.location.pathname);
            }
        }, 300);  // 300ms 지연 (폴더/세션 목록 로드 대기)
    }

    console.log('✅ App ready');
};
# File name: auth.js

// =========================
// Authentication Module
// =========================

// =========================
// Guest Access Gate
// =========================

/**
 * Check if user has valid access and show gate if not
 * Called on page load before any UI is shown
 */
async function checkAccessGate() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token');

    if (!token) {
        // No token - show access gate
        showGuestAccessModal();
        return false;
    }

    // Token exists - verify it's still valid
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/me`, {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
        });

        if (res.ok) {
            // Token valid - hide gate and continue
            hideGuestAccessModal();
            return true;
        } else {
            // Token invalid/expired - show access gate
            localStorage.removeItem('auth_token');
            AUTH_TOKEN = null;
            showGuestAccessModal();
            return false;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Auth check failed:', e);
        // Network error - show access gate
        showGuestAccessModal();
        return false;
    }
}

/**
 * Show the guest access modal (non-closable)
 */
function showGuestAccessModal() {
    document.getElementById('guestAccessModal').style.display = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('guestAccessCode').focus();
    // Hide error on show
    document.getElementById('guestAccessError').classList.add('hidden');
}

/**
 * Hide the guest access modal
 */
function hideGuestAccessModal() {
    document.getElementById('guestAccessModal').style.display = 'none';
}

/**
 * Submit guest access code
 */
async function submitGuestAccess() {
    const codeInput = document.getElementById('guestAccessCode');
    const errorDiv = document.getElementById('guestAccessError');
    const errorText = document.getElementById('guestAccessErrorText');
    const btn = document.getElementById('guestAccessBtn');
    const btnIcon = document.getElementById('guestAccessBtnIcon');
    const btnText = document.getElementById('guestAccessBtnText');

    const accessCode = codeInput.value.trim();

    if (!accessCode) {
        errorText.textContent = 'Please enter an access code';
        errorDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
        codeInput.focus();
        return;
    }

    // Show loading state
    btn.disabled = true;
    btnIcon.className = 'fas fa-spinner fa-spin';
    btnText.textContent = 'Verifying...';
    errorDiv.classList.add('hidden');

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/guest-login`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ access_code: accessCode })
        });

        if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();

            // Save token
            AUTH_TOKEN = data.access_token;
            localStorage.setItem('auth_token', AUTH_TOKEN);

            // Update UI for guest
            IS_GUEST_MODE = true;
            CURRENT_USER = { id: data.user_id, role: data.role };

            // Success - hide modal and reload to initialize properly
            hideGuestAccessModal();
            window.location.reload();

        } else {
            // Failed - show error
            const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            errorText.textContent = errorData.detail || 'Invalid access code';
            errorDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
            codeInput.value = '';
            codeInput.focus();
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Guest login error:', e);
        errorText.textContent = 'Network error. Please try again.';
        errorDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
    } finally {
        // Reset button state
        btn.disabled = false;
        btnIcon.className = 'fas fa-key';
        btnText.textContent = 'Access';
    }
}

/**
 * Switch from guest modal to login modal
 */
function showLoginFromGuestModal() {
    hideGuestAccessModal();
    showLoginModal();
}

// =========================
// Standard Authentication
// =========================

/**
 * 인증 확인 및 UI 업데이트
 */
async function checkAuth() {
  try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/me`, {headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}});
      if(res.ok) {
          CURRENT_USER = await res.json();
          IS_GUEST_MODE = false;
          document.getElementById('authButtons').style.display='none';
          document.getElementById('userInfo').style.display='flex';
          document.getElementById('username').textContent=CURRENT_USER.username;
          document.getElementById('guestBadge').style.display='none';
          document.getElementById('foldersSection').style.display='block';
          document.getElementById('uncategorizedSection').style.display='block';
          loadFolders(); loadUncategorizedSessions();
      } else softAuthExpireToGuest();
  } catch { softAuthExpireToGuest(); }
}


function showAuthWarning(msg) {
  // 너네 UI 구조에 맞춰 최소 침습: statusText 있으면 거기 찍고,
  // 없으면 alert 말고 상단바/토스트로 나중에 교체 가능
  const el = document.getElementById('statusText');
  if (el) {
    el.textContent = `⚠️ ${msg}`;
    el.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
    setTimeout(() => {
      el.textContent = 'Ready';
      el.classList.remove('text-yellow-400');
    }, 5000);
  }
}

function switchToGuestUI() {
  // "토큰 삭제" 같은 폭력 금지. UI만 게스트로.
  IS_GUEST_MODE = true;
  CURRENT_USER = null;

  document.getElementById('authButtons').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('userInfo').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('guestBadge').style.display = 'inline-block';
  document.getElementById('foldersSection').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('uncategorizedSection').style.display = 'none';
}

function softAuthExpireToGuest(msg) {
  // ✅ 자동 로그아웃 체감 방지 포인트:
  // - localStorage auth_token은 지우지 않는다 (사용자 의사 없이 삭제 금지)
  // - 런타임 AUTH_TOKEN은 null로 만들어서 이후 API 폭탄(401 연쇄)만 막는다
  AUTH_TOKEN = null;

  switchToGuestUI();
  showAuthWarning(msg || '세션이 만료되어 게스트로 전환했어. 다시 로그인하면 돼.');
}

/**
 * 모달 열기/닫기
 */
function showLoginModal() {
    document.getElementById('loginModal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function showRegisterModal() {
    document.getElementById('registerModal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeModal(id) {
    document.getElementById(id).style.display = 'none';
}

/**
 * 로그인
 */
async function login() {
    const u = document.getElementById('loginUsername').value;
    const p = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value;
    const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('username',u); fd.append('password',p);
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/token`, { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    if (res.ok) {
        const d = await res.json();
        AUTH_TOKEN = d.access_token;
        localStorage.setItem('auth_token', AUTH_TOKEN);
        closeModal('loginModal');
        hideGuestAccessModal(); // Hide guest modal if open
        // Reload to ensure clean state
        window.location.reload();
    } else {
        alert('Login Failed');
    }
}

/**
 * 회원가입
 */
async function register() {
    const u = document.getElementById('registerUsername').value;
    const p = document.getElementById('registerPassword').value;

    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/register`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username: u, password: p })
    });

    if (res.ok) {
        const d = await res.json();
        AUTH_TOKEN = d.access_token;
        localStorage.setItem('auth_token', AUTH_TOKEN);
        closeModal('registerModal');
        hideGuestAccessModal(); // Hide guest modal if open
        // Reload to ensure clean state
        window.location.reload();
    } else {
        alert('Register Failed');
    }
}

/**
 * 로그아웃
 */
function logout() {
    console.log('👋 [Logout] Cashing out and leaving...');

    // 1. 보안 토큰 파기 (가장 중요)
    AUTH_TOKEN = null;
    localStorage.removeItem('auth_token'); // 저장된 키 이름 확인! (auth_token or access_token)
    
    // 2. 세션 변수 초기화 (혹시 모를 잔여 데이터 제거)
    CURRENT_SESSION_ID = null;
    CURRENT_FOLDER_ID = null;

    // 3. 💎 [핵심] 입구로 강제 이동 (Redirect)
    // 브라우저가 페이지를 새로 불러오면서 모든 메모리/화면이 초기화됨
    window.location.href = '/ui/index.html';
    
    // 만약 "뒤로 가기"로 다시 로그인 상태로 돌아오는 걸 막고 싶다면 아래 줄을 사용해:
     window.location.replace('/ui/index.html');
}

                >
                    <i id="guestAccessBtnIcon" class="fas fa-key"></i>
                    <span id="guestAccessBtnText">Access</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            <div class="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-dark-border">
                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 text-center">
                    Already have an account?
                    <button onclick="showLoginFromGuestModal()" class="text-purple-400 hover:text-purple-300 underline">Login here</button>
                </p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ✅ JS (Single Source of Truth: assets/js) -->
    <script defer src="./assets/js/state.js"></script><!-- 상태/전역 -->
    <script defer src="./assets/js/api.js"></script><!-- fetch 래퍼 -->
    <script defer src="./assets/js/utils.js"></script><!-- 유틸리티 -->
    <script defer src="./assets/js/ui-render.js"></script><!-- UI 렌더링 -->

# File name: chat.js

// =========================
