async function loadFolders() {
    if(!AUTH_TOKEN) return;
    const res=await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders`,{headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}});
    if(res.ok) { FOLDERS=await res.json(); renderFolders(); }
}

function renderFolders() {
    const list=document.getElementById('foldersList'); list.innerHTML='';
    FOLDERS.forEach(f=>{
        const div=document.createElement('div'); div.className='bg-dark-hover rounded-lg overflow-hidden';
        div.innerHTML=`<div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 hover:bg-dark-border"><div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1 cursor-pointer" onclick="toggleFolderAccordion(${f.id})"><span class="text-xl">${f.icon}</span><div><div class="font-medium text-sm">${escapeHtml(f.name)}</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">${f.session_count} sessions</div></div></div><div class="flex items-center gap-1"><button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); showFolderSettings(${f.id})" class="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-purple-400"><i class="fas fa-cog text-sm"></i></button><button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleFolderAccordion(${f.id})" class="p-2"><span id="folderIcon${f.id}">▼</span></button></div></div><div id="folderSessions${f.id}" class="accordion-content"><div class="p-2 space-y-1" id="sessionsList${f.id}"><button onclick="selectFolder(${f.id})" class="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 rounded text-sm">+ New Chat in ${escapeHtml(f.name)}</button></div></div>`;
        list.appendChild(div); loadFolderSessions(f.id);
    });
}

async function loadFolderSessions(fid) {
    const res=await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders/${fid}/sessions`,{headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}});
    if(res.ok) {
        const s=await res.json(); const c=document.getElementById(`sessionsList${fid}`);
        s.forEach(ss=>{
            const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='group relative px-3 py-2 bg-dark-bg hover:bg-dark-border rounded text-sm cursor-pointer truncate flex items-center justify-between';
            // ✅ [FIX] folder_id 전달하여 CURRENT_FOLDER 보존
            d.innerHTML=`<div class="flex-1 truncate" onclick="loadSession(${ss.id}, ${fid})"><div class="font-medium truncate">${escapeHtml(ss.title)}</div></div><button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); deleteSession(${ss.id},${fid})" class="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 p-1 hover:bg-red-600 rounded text-gray-400 hover:text-white"><i class="fas fa-trash text-xs"></i></button>`;
            c.appendChild(d);
        });
    }
}


function selectFolder(id) {
    // ✅ [SESSION PERSISTENCE] URL 파라미터 제거 (새 채팅 in 폴더)
    history.pushState(null, '', window.location.pathname);
    console.log(`🔗 [URL] Cleared session_id (new chat in folder ${id})`);

    CURRENT_FOLDER_ID=id;
    CURRENT_SESSION_ID=null;
    TEMP_SESSION_ID=null;
    CURRENT_FOLDER=FOLDERS.find(x=>x.id===id);
    document.getElementById('chatMessages').innerHTML='<div class="text-center text-gray-500 py-8"><p class="text-xl">New Chat in '+escapeHtml(CURRENT_FOLDER.name)+'</p></div>';
    document.getElementById('sessionInfo').textContent=`Folder: ${CURRENT_FOLDER.name}`;
    document.getElementById('ragStatus').classList.remove('hidden');
}

 
function newChat() {
    // ✅ [SESSION PERSISTENCE] URL 파라미터 제거 (새 채팅)
    history.pushState(null, '', window.location.pathname);
    console.log(`🔗 [URL] Cleared session_id (new chat)`);

    CURRENT_SESSION_ID=null;
    CURRENT_FOLDER_ID=null;
    document.getElementById('chatMessages').innerHTML='<div class="text-center text-gray-500 py-8">New Chat</div>';
    document.getElementById('sessionInfo').textContent='';
}

async function loadUncategorizedSessions() {
    if(!AUTH_TOKEN) return;
    const res=await fetch(`${API_BASE}/chat/sessions/uncategorized`,{headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}});
    if(res.ok) {
        const s=await res.json(); const c=document.getElementById('uncategorizedList'); c.innerHTML='';
        if(s.length===0) c.innerHTML='<div class="text-xs text-gray-500 p-2">No sessions</div>';
        s.forEach(ss=>{
            const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='group bg-dark-hover rounded p-2 cursor-pointer flex justify-between';
            d.innerHTML=`<div onclick="loadSession(${ss.id})" class="truncate flex-1">${escapeHtml(ss.title)}</div><button onclick="deleteSession(${ss.id})" class="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-red-400"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>`;
            c.appendChild(d);
        });
    }
}


function toggleUncategorized() { 
    const l=document.getElementById('uncategorizedList'); 
    l.style.display=l.style.display==='none'?'block':'none'; 
}

async function createFolder() {
    const n=document.getElementById('folderName').value; const p=document.getElementById('folderSystemPrompt').value;
    const r=document.getElementById('folderUseRAG').checked; const c=document.getElementById('folderIsCreative').checked;
    const i=document.getElementById('selectedIcon').value;
    await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({name:n,system_prompt:p,use_rag:r,icon:i,is_creative:c})});
    closeModal('createFolderModal'); loadFolders();
}

function showCreateFolderModal() { 
    if(IS_GUEST_MODE) return alert('Login required'); 
    document.getElementById('createFolderModal').style.display='flex'; 
}

function toggleFolderAccordion(id) { 
    const c=document.getElementById(`folderSessions${id}`); 
    c.classList.toggle('open'); 
}

async function deleteSession(sid, fid=null) {
    if(confirm('Delete session?')) {
        await fetch(`${API_BASE}/chat/sessions/${sid}`,{method:'DELETE',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}});
        if(fid) loadFolders();
        else loadUncategorizedSessions();

        // ✅ [SESSION PERSISTENCE] 현재 세션 삭제 시 URL 파라미터도 제거
        if(CURRENT_SESSION_ID===sid) {
            newChat();  // 이미 URL 파라미터 제거 포함됨
        }
    }
}

// Simplified versions of other folder functions
// Folder Settings & Docs (원본 유지)
async function showFolderSettings(fid) {
    CURRENT_FOLDER_SETTINGS_ID=fid; const f=FOLDERS.find(x=>x.id===fid); if(!f) return;
    document.getElementById('folderSettingsIcon').textContent=f.icon; document.getElementById('editFolderName').value=f.name;
    document.getElementById('editFolderPrompt').value=f.system_prompt; document.getElementById('editFolderIsCreative').checked=f.is_creative;
    loadFolderDocuments(fid); document.getElementById('folderSettingsModal').style.display='flex';
}
async function loadFolderDocuments(fid) {
    const c=document.getElementById('folderDocumentsList'); c.innerHTML='Loading...';
    const res=await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders/${fid}/documents`,{headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}});
    if(res.ok) {
        const docs=await res.json(); c.innerHTML=docs.length?docs.map(d=>`<div class="flex justify-between p-2 bg-dark-hover mb-1 rounded"><span>${escapeHtml(d.filename)}</span><button onclick="deleteFolderDocument(${fid},${d.id})" class="text-red-400"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button></div>`).join(''):'No docs';
    }
}
async function uploadToCurrentFolder(input) {
    const f=input.files[0]; if(!f || !CURRENT_FOLDER_SETTINGS_ID) return;
    const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('file',f);
    await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders/${CURRENT_FOLDER_SETTINGS_ID}/upload`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`},body:fd});
    loadFolderDocuments(CURRENT_FOLDER_SETTINGS_ID);
}
async function deleteFolderDocument(fid, did) {
    if(confirm('Delete?')) { await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders/${fid}/documents/${did}`,{method:'DELETE',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}}); loadFolderDocuments(fid); }
}
async function saveFolderSettings() {
    const n=document.getElementById('editFolderName').value; const p=document.getElementById('editFolderPrompt').value; const c=document.getElementById('editFolderIsCreative').checked;
    await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders/${CURRENT_FOLDER_SETTINGS_ID}`,{method:'PATCH',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({name:n,system_prompt:p,is_creative:c})});
    closeModal('folderSettingsModal'); loadFolders();
}
async function confirmDeleteFolder() {
    if(confirm('Delete folder?')) { await fetch(`${API_BASE}/folders/${CURRENT_FOLDER_SETTINGS_ID}`,{method:'DELETE',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${AUTH_TOKEN}`}}); closeModal('folderSettingsModal'); loadFolders(); }
}

function selectEmoji(el,v) { document.querySelectorAll('.emoji-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('selected')); el.classList.add('selected'); document.getElementById('selectedIcon').value=v; }

// =========================

/**
 * 전송 버튼 상태 업데이트
 */
function updateSendButtonState(generating) {
    const btn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
    const icon = document.getElementById('sendIcon');
    if(generating) {
        btn.classList.replace('bg-purple-600', 'bg-red-600');
        btn.classList.replace('hover:bg-purple-700', 'hover:bg-red-700');
        icon.classList.replace('fa-paper-plane', 'fa-stop');
    } else {
        btn.classList.replace('bg-red-600', 'bg-purple-600');
        btn.classList.replace('hover:bg-red-700', 'hover:bg-purple-700');
        icon.classList.replace('fa-stop', 'fa-paper-plane');
    }
}

// ✅ [Edit] 옵션 B: 수정 모드 경고 바 표시/숨김
function showEditWarningBar({ text, onCancel }) {
    const bar = document.getElementById('editWarningBar');
    if (!bar) return;
  
    bar.innerHTML = `
      <div class="edit-warning-text">${escapeHtml(text)}</div>
      <button id="editCancelBtn" class="edit-warning-cancel">수정 취소</button>
    `;
    bar.style.display = 'flex';
  
    const btn = document.getElementById('editCancelBtn');
    btn.onclick = onCancel;
  }
  
  function hideEditWarningBar() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('editWarningBar');
    if (!bar) return;
    bar.style.display = 'none';
    bar.innerHTML = '';
  }

// [중요] 메시지 렌더링 (시간 표시 & 재생성 & 스마트 카피 복구 & 사용자 메시지 수정)
function addMessageToUI(role, content, ragUsed=false, messageId=null, feedbackPositive=null, autoSelected=false, selectedMode=null, timeTaken=null, originMid=null) {
    const container = document.getElementById('chatMessages');
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = `flex ${role==='user'?'justify-end':'justify-start'} mb-4`;

    let processed = content;
    if(role==='assistant' && content.includes('<think>')) {
        const parts = content.split('</think>');
        // ✅ [P0] DOM 구조 안정성: thought-content 클래스 추가 (switchVersion에서 사용)
        processed = `<details class="mb-3 opacity-60 text-xs border-b border-gray-700 pb-2 w-full"><summary class="cursor-pointer hover:text-purple-400">🤔 생각 과정</summary><div class="thought-content p-3 mt-2 bg-black bg-opacity-40 rounded italic whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(parts[0].replace('<think>','').trim())}</div></details><div class="actual-answer whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(parts[1]?parts[1].trim():"")}</div>`;
    } else {
        // ✅ [P0] DOM 구조 안정성: assistant 메시지에 actual-answer 클래스 보장
        processed = role === 'assistant'
            ? `<div class="actual-answer whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(content)}</div>`
            : `<div class="whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(content)}</div>`;
    }

    // ✅ [A안] dataset.originMid 속성 추가 (선행 공백 포함하여 속성 분리 보장)
    const originMidAttr = (role === 'assistant' && originMid) ? ` data-origin-mid="${String(originMid)}"` : '';
    // ✅ [Edit] dataset.messageId 속성 추가 (서버 동기화용)
    const messageIdAttr = messageId ? ` data-message-id="${messageId}"` : '';
    // ✅ [FIX-1A] data-mid, data-role 추가 (Abort 메시지 Regenerate 지원)
    const midAttr = messageId !== null ? ` data-mid="${messageId}"` : ' data-mid="null"';
    const roleAttr = ` data-role="${role}"`;
    let html = `<div class="max-w-[85%] rounded-xl px-4 py-3 ${role==='user'?'message-user text-white':'message-assistant text-gray-100 shadow-lg'} relative group"${originMidAttr}${messageIdAttr}${midAttr}${roleAttr}>`;

    // ========================================
    // [NEW] 사용자 메시지: 수정 버튼 추가 (좌측 상단으로 이동)
    // ========================================
    if (role === 'user') {
        html += `
            <button onclick="startEditMessage(this)"
                    class="absolute top-2 -left-7 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity p-1.5 bg-black bg-opacity-30 hover:bg-opacity-50 rounded-lg text-white shadow-lg"
                    title="Edit Message">
                <i class="fas fa-edit text-sm"></i>
            </button>
        `;
    }

    html += processed;

    // ========================================
    // Assistant 메시지: 기존 피드백 버튼 유지
    // ========================================
    if(role==='assistant') {
        html += `<div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">`;
        // [복구] 시간 표시 (서버 시간 또는 클라이언트 계산 시간)
        if(timeTaken) html += `<span class="py-1 px-2 bg-gray-800 bg-opacity-50 rounded text-[10px] text-gray-400 border border-gray-600 font-mono">⏱️ ${parseFloat(timeTaken).toFixed(2)}s</span>`;
        if(selectedMode) html += `<span class="py-1 px-2 bg-gray-700 bg-opacity-50 rounded text-[10px] border">🤖 ${autoSelected?"Auto→":""}${selectedMode.toUpperCase()}</span>`;
        if(ragUsed) html += `<span class="py-1 px-2 bg-purple-900 bg-opacity-50 rounded text-[10px] text-purple-200 border border-purple-700">📚 RAG</span>`;
        html += `</div>`;

        // 버튼들
        const upColor = feedbackPositive===true?'text-green-400':'text-gray-500';
        const downColor = feedbackPositive===false?'text-red-400':'text-gray-500';
        html += `<div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 pt-2 border-t border-gray-700/50"><button onclick="copyToClipboard(this)" class="text-xs text-gray-500 hover:text-white" title="Copy Text"><i class="fas fa-copy"></i></button>`;
        if(messageId) html += `<div class="flex gap-2 border-l border-gray-700 pl-3"><button onclick="submitFeedback(${messageId},true,this)" class="text-xs ${upColor} hover:text-green-400"><i class="fas fa-thumbs-up"></i></button><button onclick="submitFeedback(${messageId},false,this)" class="text-xs ${downColor} hover:text-red-400"><i class="fas fa-thumbs-down"></i></button></div>`;
        // ... (위쪽 코드: 복사 버튼, 피드백 버튼 등) ...
        
        // 1. 💎 버튼을 담을 '우측 정렬 그릇' (하나만 만든다!)
        html += `<div class="ml-auto flex gap-2">`;

        // 🔒 2. Regenerate 버튼: 소설방(is_creative) VIP 전용
        // (불필요한 div 태그 제거하고 button만 깔끔하게 넣음)
        if (CURRENT_FOLDER && CURRENT_FOLDER.is_creative) {
             html += `<button onclick="regenerateResponse(${messageId}, this)" class="btn-regenerate text-xs text-gray-500 hover:text-purple-400" title="Regenerate" style="display:none;"><i class="fas fa-sync-alt"></i></button>`;
        }
        // 🌍 3. Continue 버튼: 누구나 사용 가능 (조건 없음)
        html += `<button onclick="continueResponse(${messageId}, this)" class="btn-continue text-xs text-gray-500 hover:text-blue-400" title="Continue" style="display:none;"><i class="fas fa-arrow-right"></i></button>`;
        // 4. 그릇 닫기
        html += `</div>`;
    }
    html += `</div>`;
    div.innerHTML = html;
    container.appendChild(div);

    // ✅ [Regenerate] assistant 메시지 추가 후 버튼 가시성 업데이트
    if (role === 'assistant') {
        setTimeout(() => updateRegenerateVisibility({ showOnly: div }), 0);
    }
}

/**
 * Regenerate 버튼 가시성 제어
 * @param {Object} options - { hideAll: boolean, showOnly: HTMLElement }
 */
function updateRegenerateVisibility(options = {}) {
    const { hideAll = false, showOnly = null } = options;

    // 모든 버튼 찾기
    const allRegenerateButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.btn-regenerate');
    const allContinueButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.btn-continue');

    if (hideAll) {
        // 모두 숨김
        allRegenerateButtons.forEach(btn => btn.style.display = 'none');
        allContinueButtons.forEach(btn => btn.style.display = 'none');
        return;
    }

    if (showOnly) {
        // 1. 모든 버튼 숨김
        allRegenerateButtons.forEach(btn => btn.style.display = 'none');
        allContinueButtons.forEach(btn => btn.style.display = 'none');

        // 2. 지정된 버블의 regenerate 버튼만 표시
        const targetButton = showOnly.querySelector('.btn-regenerate');
        if (targetButton) {
            targetButton.style.display = 'inline-block';
        }
        const targetContinueBtn = showOnly.querySelector('.btn-continue');
        if (targetContinueBtn) {
            targetContinueBtn.style.display = 'inline-block';
        }
    }
}

// [중요] 스마트 카피 기능 복구 (아이콘 변경 + 내용만 복사)
async function copyToClipboard(btn) {
    try {
        const messageBubble = btn.closest('[data-role="assistant"]') || btn.closest('.message-assistant'); if (!messageBubble) return;

        let txt = "";
        // 1. 답변만 추출 (생각 과정 제외)
        const actualAnswerDiv = messageBubble.querySelector('.actual-answer');
        if (actualAnswerDiv) txt = actualAnswerDiv.innerText;
        else {
            const simpleTextDiv = messageBubble.querySelector('.whitespace-pre-wrap');
            if(simpleTextDiv) txt = simpleTextDiv.innerText;
            else txt = messageBubble.innerText; // Fallback
        }

        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
        
        // 2. 아이콘 변경 애니메이션
        const icon = btn.querySelector('i');
        const originalClass = icon.className;
        icon.className = 'fas fa-check text-green-400';
        setTimeout(() => icon.className = originalClass, 2000);
    } catch(e) { console.error(e); alert('Copy failed'); }
}

/**
 * 버전 관리 (간소화)
 */
function findBubbleByOriginMid(originMid) {
    return document.querySelector(`[data-origin-mid="${originMid}"]`);
}

function attachOrUpdateVersionControls(bubbleEl, originMid) {
    if (!answerVersions[originMid] || answerVersions[originMid].items.length <= 1) {
        // 버전이 1개 이하면 토글 UI 숨김
        const existingControls = bubbleEl.querySelector('.version-controls');
        if (existingControls) existingControls.remove();
        return;
    }

    const versions = answerVersions[originMid];
    const currentIndex = versions.index;
    const totalCount = versions.items.length;

    // 기존 컨트롤 제거 (업데이트용)
    const existingControls = bubbleEl.querySelector('.version-controls');
    if (existingControls) existingControls.remove();

    // content div 찾기 (actual-answer 또는 whitespace-pre-wrap)
    const contentDiv = bubbleEl.querySelector('.actual-answer') || bubbleEl.querySelector('.whitespace-pre-wrap');
    if (!contentDiv) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ [Version] Could not find content div for originMid=${originMid}`);
        return;
    }

    // 버전 토글 UI 생성
    const controlsDiv = document.createElement('div');
    controlsDiv.className = 'version-controls mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-gray-700 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-gray-400';
    controlsDiv.innerHTML = `
        <button class="version-prev px-2 py-1 rounded hover:bg-gray-700 transition ${currentIndex === 0 ? 'opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}"
                ${currentIndex === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}
                onclick="switchVersion('${originMid}', -1)">
            <i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i>
        </button>
        <span class="version-counter font-mono">${currentIndex + 1} / ${totalCount}</span>
        <button class="version-next px-2 py-1 rounded hover:bg-gray-700 transition ${currentIndex === totalCount - 1 ? 'opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}"
                ${currentIndex === totalCount - 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}
                onclick="switchVersion('${originMid}', 1)">
            <i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i>
        </button>
    `;

    // 버블 끝에 추가
    bubbleEl.appendChild(controlsDiv);
    console.log(`🔁 [Version] Attached controls for originMid=${originMid} (${currentIndex + 1}/${totalCount})`);
}

function switchVersion(originMid, direction) {
    const versions = answerVersions[originMid];
    if (!versions) return;

    // 인덱스 변경
    const newIndex = versions.index + direction;
    if (newIndex < 0 || newIndex >= versions.items.length) return;

    versions.index = newIndex;
    const versionData = versions.items[newIndex];

    console.log(`🔁 [Version] originMid=${originMid} -> ${newIndex + 1}/${versions.items.length}`);

    // 버블 찾기
    const bubbleEl = findBubbleByOriginMid(originMid);
    if (!bubbleEl) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ [Version] Bubble not found for originMid=${originMid}`);
        return;
    }

    // content div 찾기
    const contentDiv = bubbleEl.querySelector('.actual-answer') || bubbleEl.querySelector('.whitespace-pre-wrap');
    if (!contentDiv) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ [Version] Content div not found for originMid=${originMid}`);
        return;
    }

    // <think> 태그 처리
    let displayContent = versionData.content;
    if (displayContent.includes('<think>')) {
        const parts = displayContent.split('</think>');
        const thinkContent = parts[0].replace('<think>', '').trim();
        const answerContent = parts[1] ? parts[1].trim() : '';

        // 부모 버블에서 details와 actual-answer를 모두 업데이트
        const thoughtDetails = bubbleEl.querySelector('details');
        if (thoughtDetails) {
            const thoughtContentDiv = thoughtDetails.querySelector('.thought-content');
            if (thoughtContentDiv) thoughtContentDiv.textContent = thinkContent;
        }
        contentDiv.textContent = answerContent;
    } else {
        contentDiv.textContent = displayContent;
    }

    // 토글 UI 업데이트
    attachOrUpdateVersionControls(bubbleEl, originMid);
}


/**
 * 사이드바 토글
 */
function toggleSidebar() { 
    const s=document.getElementById('sidebar'); 
    SIDEBAR_COLLAPSED=!SIDEBAR_COLLAPSED; 
    s.className=SIDEBAR_COLLAPSED?'sidebar-collapsed bg-dark-card border-l border-dark-border transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col overflow-hidden':'sidebar-expanded bg-dark-card border-l border-dark-border transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col overflow-hidden'; 
}

// =========================

/**
 * HTML 이스케이프
 */
function escapeHtml(t) {
    return t ? t.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;") : "";
}

/**
 * 온도 슬라이더 값 표시 업데이트
 */
function updateTempDisplay() {
    document.getElementById('tempValue').textContent =
        (document.getElementById('tempSlider').value / 100).toFixed(2);
}


// ✅ [P0] 반복 루프 자동 감지 및 강제 중단
function detectRepetitionLoop(newChunk) {
    loopDetectionBuffer += newChunk;

    // 버퍼가 너무 길어지면 앞부분 제거 (최근 500자만 유지)
    if (loopDetectionBuffer.length > 500) {
        loopDetectionBuffer = loopDetectionBuffer.slice(-500);
    }

    // 동일 문구 반복 감지 (4-gram 기반)
    const words = loopDetectionBuffer.split(/\s+/);
    if (words.length < 8) return false;  // 최소 8단어 필요

    // 최근 4단어 추출
    const recentPhrase = words.slice(-4).join(' ');
    if (recentPhrase.length < 10) return false;  // 너무 짧은 문구는 무시

    // 버퍼 내에서 해당 문구 출현 횟수 계산
    const regex = new RegExp(recentPhrase.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'), 'g');
    const matches = loopDetectionBuffer.match(regex);
    const repeatCount = matches ? matches.length : 0;

    if (repeatCount >= loopDetectionThreshold) {
        console.warn(`⛔ [Loop Detection] Phrase repeated ${repeatCount} times: "${recentPhrase}"`);
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

# File name: auth.py

# auth.py - API Key and Role-Based Authentication Logic
from datetime import timedelta
import logging
from fastapi import HTTPException, Security, Depends, status
from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from config import get_settings
from database import (
    get_db, User, UserRole,
    create_access_token, get_or_create_guest_user,
    get_current_user as db_get_current_user
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# =============================================================================
# Custom Exceptions for Authentication Interruption
# =============================================================================

class AuthenticationInterruptedError(Exception):
    """Raised when authentication process is cancelled or aborted."""
    pass


class AuthenticationAbortedError(AuthenticationInterruptedError):
    """Raised when user explicitly aborts authentication."""
    pass


class AuthenticationCancelledError(AuthenticationInterruptedError):
    """Raised when authentication is cancelled (e.g., timeout, client disconnect)."""
    pass

# =============================================================================
# API Key Authentication (for external access)
# =============================================================================

api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(
    name="x-api-key",
    auto_error=False,
    description="API Key for external access authentication"
)


async def verify_api_key(api_key: str = Security(api_key_header)) -> str:
    """
    Dependency to verify the API key from request header.

    Raises:
        HTTPException: 403 Forbidden if API key is missing or invalid
    """
    settings = get_settings()

    if api_key is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="API key is missing. Please provide 'x-api-key' header."
        )

    if api_key != settings.api_key:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Invalid API key."
        )

    return api_key


def get_api_key_dependency():
    """Returns the API key verification dependency for use in routers."""
    return verify_api_key


# =============================================================================
# Guest Authentication
# =============================================================================

def verify_guest_access_code(access_code: str) -> bool:
    """Verify the guest access code."""
    settings = get_settings()
    return access_code == settings.guest_access_code


def create_guest_token(db: Session) -> dict:
    """
    Create a guest user and return access token.

    Returns:
        dict: {"access_token": str, "token_type": "bearer", "user_id": int, "role": "guest"}

    Raises:
        HTTPException: 503 if guest creation is interrupted or fails
        HTTPException: 401 if authentication process is aborted
    """
    settings = get_settings()
    guest_user = None

    try:
        # Create a new guest user
        guest_user = get_or_create_guest_user(db)

        if guest_user is None:
            raise AuthenticationCancelledError("Guest user creation returned None")

        # Create token with guest role
        access_token = create_access_token(
            data={"sub": guest_user.username},
            expires_delta=timedelta(hours=settings.guest_token_expire_hours),
            role=UserRole.GUEST.value
        )

        if not access_token:
            raise AuthenticationCancelledError("Token creation failed")

        return {
            "access_token": access_token,
            "token_type": "bearer",
            "user_id": guest_user.id,
            "role": UserRole.GUEST.value
        }

    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as e:
        # User cancelled (Ctrl+C) or system abort
        logger.warning(f"[Auth] Guest login aborted: {type(e).__name__}")
        _rollback_guest_user(db, guest_user)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Authentication aborted by user"
        )

    except SQLAlchemyError as e:
        # Database error during authentication
        logger.error(f"[Auth] DB error during guest login: {e}")
        _rollback_guest_user(db, guest_user)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Authentication service temporarily unavailable"
        )

    except AuthenticationInterruptedError as e:
        logger.warning(f"[Auth] Guest login interrupted: {e}")
        _rollback_guest_user(db, guest_user)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Authentication was interrupted"
        )

    except Exception as e:
        # Catch-all for unexpected errors - prevent partial auth state
        logger.error(f"[Auth] Unexpected error during guest login: {e}")
        _rollback_guest_user(db, guest_user)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="Authentication failed unexpectedly"
        )


def _rollback_guest_user(db: Session, guest_user) -> None:
    """Rollback partially created guest user to prevent orphaned records."""
    try:
        if guest_user and hasattr(guest_user, 'id') and guest_user.id:
            db.rollback()
            # Attempt to delete if already committed
            existing = db.query(User).filter(User.id == guest_user.id).first()
            if existing:
                db.delete(existing)
                db.commit()
                logger.info(f"[Auth] Rolled back guest user: {guest_user.id}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"[Auth] Failed to rollback guest user: {e}")
        db.rollback()


# =============================================================================
# Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Dependencies
# =============================================================================

def require_role(*allowed_roles: str):
    """
    Dependency factory to require specific roles.

    Usage:
        @app.get("/admin-only", dependencies=[Depends(require_role("admin"))])
        async def admin_only():
            return {"message": "Admin access"}

        @app.get("/admin-or-user", dependencies=[Depends(require_role("admin", "user"))])
        async def admin_or_user():
            return {"message": "Admin or User access"}
    """
    async def role_checker(user: User = Depends(db_get_current_user)):
        if user.role not in allowed_roles:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail=f"Access denied. Required role: {', '.join(allowed_roles)}. Your role: {user.role}"
            )
        return user
    return role_checker


def require_admin():
    """Dependency to require admin role."""
    return require_role(UserRole.ADMIN.value)


def require_user_or_admin():
    """Dependency to require user or admin role (excludes guest)."""
    return require_role(UserRole.ADMIN.value, UserRole.USER.value)

except ImportError:
    RAG_AVAILABLE = False

# [3] Web Search 엔진 가져오기
try:
    from web_ops import search_web
    WEB_SEARCH_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    WEB_SEARCH_AVAILABLE = False

# State Machine
try:
    from state_machine import create_state_machine
    STATE_MACHINE_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    STATE_MACHINE_AVAILABLE = False

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ==========================================
# Hybrid Dual Model Loading (Fast + Main)
# Uses ModelService for centralized GPU management
# ==========================================
MAIN_MODEL_PATH = "models/Qwen3-14B-Q4_K_M.gguf"  # Thinking/Research용 고성능 모델
FAST_MODEL_PATH = "models/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf"  # Fast 모드용 경량 모델

# Model references (now managed by ModelService)
main_model = None
fast_model = None

if MODEL_SERVICE_AVAILABLE:
    # Models are loaded lazily by ModelService
    print("✅ [GPU Concurrency] ModelService available - using asyncio.Lock for inference")
    print("   └─ Single inference at a time, others queue automatically")

    # Get model references from service (lazy loaded)
    def _get_models():
        global main_model, fast_model
        main_model = get_main_model()
        fast_model = get_fast_model()

    # Defer loading until first use
    _get_models()

elif LLAMA_CPP_AVAILABLE:
    # Fallback: Direct loading without service
    print("⚠️ [GPU Concurrency] ModelService not available - using fallback lock")

    if os.path.exists(MAIN_MODEL_PATH):
        print(f"🔄 Loading Main Model: {MAIN_MODEL_PATH} ...")
        print(f"   └─ Hardware: RTX 5070 Ti (16GB VRAM) + 64GB RAM")
        try:
            main_model = Llama(
                model_path=MAIN_MODEL_PATH,
                n_ctx=16384,
                n_gpu_layers=-1,
                verbose=False
            )
            print("✅ Main Model (Qwen 14B) Loaded! [n_ctx=16384]")
        except MemoryError as me:
            print(f"❌ Main Model Load Failed (VRAM Insufficient): {me}")
            main_model = None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Main Model Load Failed: {e}")
            main_model = None
    else:
        print(f"⚠️ Main Model not found: {MAIN_MODEL_PATH}")

    if os.path.exists(FAST_MODEL_PATH):
        print(f"🔄 Loading Fast Model: {FAST_MODEL_PATH} ...")
        try:
            fast_model = Llama(
                model_path=FAST_MODEL_PATH,
                n_ctx=4096,
                n_gpu_layers=-1,
                verbose=False
            )
            print("✅ Fast Model (Qwen 3B) Loaded! [n_ctx=4096]")
        except MemoryError as me:
            print(f"❌ Fast Model Load Failed (VRAM Insufficient): {me}")
            fast_model = None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Fast Model Load Failed: {e}")
            fast_model = None
    else:
        print(f"⚠️ Fast Model not found: {FAST_MODEL_PATH}")
else:
    print("⚠️ llama-cpp-python not available")

# --- Schemas ---
class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    question: str
    session_id: Optional[int] = None
    folder_id: Optional[int] = None
    temp_session_id: Optional[str] = None
    mode: str = "thinking"  # "thinking" (기본, 심층 사고) 또는 "fast" (빠른 모드)
    skip_user_message: bool = False  # True면 user 메시지를 DB에 저장하지 않음 (Continue/Regenerate용) 

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    answer: str
    session_id: int
    message_id: int
    state: str = "COMPLETED"
    rag_used: bool = False
    auto_selected: bool = False  # Auto 모드에서 자동 선택 여부
    selected_mode: Optional[str] = None  # 실제 사용된 모드 (fast/thinking/research)
    processing_time: Optional[float] = None  # 응답 생성 시간 (초)

class SessionCreate(BaseModel):
    folder_id: Optional[int] = None
    title: Optional[str] = None

class SessionResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    folder_id: Optional[int]
    title: str
    created_at: datetime
    message_count: int = 0
    class Config: from_attributes = True

class MessageResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    role: str
    content: str
    timestamp: datetime
    rag_used: bool = False
    feedback_positive: Optional[bool] = None  # 피드백 상태
    auto_selected: bool = False  # Auto 모드 사용 여부
    selected_mode: Optional[str] = None  # 실제 사용된 모드
    processing_time: Optional[float] = None  # 응답 생성 시간
    class Config: from_attributes = True

class FeedbackRequest(BaseModel):
    is_positive: bool
    comment: Optional[str] = None

class RestoreMessageItem(BaseModel):
    role: str
    content: str
    rag_used: bool = False
    auto_selected: bool = False
    selected_mode: Optional[str] = None
    processing_time: Optional[float] = None
    timestamp: Optional[str] = None

class RestoreMessagesRequest(BaseModel):
    messages: List[RestoreMessageItem]

# --- Helpers ---
def auto_title(content): return content[:30] + "..." if len(content) > 30 else content

def should_use_main_model(question: str) -> bool:
    """
    Auto 모드에서 Main 모델(Qwen 14B)을 사용해야 하는지 판단

    우선순위:
    1. 키워드 매칭 (분석, 리포트, 전망, 전략 등) → Main 모델
    2. 단순 감탄사/이모티콘만 있음 → Fast 모델
    3. 50자 미만 → Fast 모델
    4. 50자 이상 → Main 모델
    """
    import re

    # 1. 깊은 사고가 필요한 키워드 검사
    deep_thinking_keywords = [
        '분석', '리포트', '전망', '전략', '계획', '설계',
        '비교', '평가', '검토', '연구', '조사', '탐구',
        'analysis', 'report', 'strategy', 'plan', 'research',
        'compare', 'evaluate', 'review', 'investigate'
    ]

    question_lower = question.lower()
    for keyword in deep_thinking_keywords:
        if keyword in question_lower:
            print(f"🧠 [Auto Mode] Keyword '{keyword}' detected → Main Model (deep thinking required)")
            return True

    # 2. 단순 감탄사/이모티콘 검사 (한글 자음 반복, ㅋㅋ, ㅎㅎ 등)
    # 자음만 있거나 이모티콘이 대부분인 경우 Fast 모델
    hangul_consonants = re.findall(r'[ㅋㅎㄷㄱㅅㅈㅂㄴㅁㅇㄹ]+', question)
    emoticons = re.findall(r'[😀-🙏💀-💯🔥⭐✨🎉🎊👍👎❤️💙💚🤔🤣😂😭😅]+', question)

    # 질문의 대부분이 자음이나 이모티콘인 경우
    consonant_ratio = sum(len(c) for c in hangul_consonants) / max(len(question), 1)
    emoticon_ratio = sum(len(e) for e in emoticons) / max(len(question), 1)

    if consonant_ratio > 0.5 or emoticon_ratio > 0.3:
        print(f"🎭 [Auto Mode] Simple exclamation/emoticon detected → Fast Model (casual chat)")
        return False

    # 3. 길이 기준 (50자)
    if len(question) < 50:
        return False  # Fast 모델
    else:
        return True  # Main 모델

def truncate_to_token_limit(text: str, max_chars: int = 12000) -> str:
    """
    텍스트를 대략적인 토큰 제한에 맞춰 자릅니다.
    (1 토큰 ≈ 4 chars 가정, 16K ctx의 75% = 12K tokens ≈ 48K chars)
    안전 마진을 위해 12K chars로 제한
    """
    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return text

    truncated = text[:max_chars]
    return truncated + "\n\n[... 결과가 너무 길어 일부 생략되었습니다 ...]"

def hybrid_generate(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode="thinking", question_text=""):
    """
    Hybrid Dual Model Generation Engine
    - mode="fast": 빠른 응답용 경량 모델 사용 (Qwen 3B)
    - mode="thinking": 심층 사고용 고성능 모델 사용 (Qwen 14B)
    - mode="research": 웹 검색 + 고성능 모델 (Qwen 14B)
    - mode="auto": 질문 길이 기반 자동 선택

    Args:
        question_text: 순수 사용자 질문 (Auto 모드 판단용)
    """
    # ========================================
    # [RESEARCH MODE] 웹 검색 결과 주입 (고사양 최적화)
    # ========================================
    if mode == "research":
        if WEB_SEARCH_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                print(f"🌐 [Research Mode] Performing web search for: '{user_prompt[:100]}...'")
                web_results = search_web(user_prompt, max_results=5)

                # 토큰 제한 관리: 웹 검색 결과가 너무 길면 자르기
                web_results_truncated = truncate_to_token_limit(web_results, max_chars=12000)

                if len(web_results) != len(web_results_truncated):
                    print(f"⚠️ [Research Mode] Web results truncated: {len(web_results)} → {len(web_results_truncated)} chars")

                # 웹 검색 결과를 프롬프트에 명확하게 주입
                enhanced_user_prompt = f"""=== Web Search Results ===
{web_results_truncated}

=== User Question ===
{user_prompt}

=== Instruction ===
Answer the user's question by combining:
1. The web search results above (for up-to-date, external information)
2. Your own knowledge and reasoning

Provide a comprehensive, well-structured answer."""

                user_prompt = enhanced_user_prompt
                print(f"✅ [Research Mode] Web results injected into prompt (total length: {len(user_prompt)} chars)")

            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Research mode web search failed: {e}")
                # 웹 검색 실패 시에도 계속 진행 (일반 모드로 폴백)
                user_prompt = f"[Note: Web search failed] {user_prompt}"
        else:
            logger.warning("⚠️ Research mode requested but web search not available")
            user_prompt = f"[Note: Web search unavailable] {user_prompt}"

    # ========================================
    # 모델 선택 (Intelligent Auto Mode) - 고사양 최적화
    # ========================================
    if mode == "fast":
        # Fast Mode: 무조건 경량 모델
        selected_model = fast_model
        model_name = "Fast (Llama 3B)"
        max_tokens = 2048  # 고사양 최적화: 1024 → 2048 (n_ctx=4096의 50%)

    elif mode == "auto":
        # Auto Mode: 순수 질문 길이 기반 자동 선택
        # user_prompt가 아닌 question_text(순수 질문)의 길이로 판단
        question_length = len(question_text) if question_text else len(user_prompt)

        if question_length < 15:  # 15자 미만 → Fast
            # 짧은 질문 → 빠른 모델
            selected_model = fast_model
            model_name = "Fast (Llama 3B)"
            max_tokens = 2048  # 고사양 최적화: 1024 → 2048
            print(f"🤖 [Auto Mode] Question length={question_length} chars → Using Fast Model (short question)")
        else:
            # 긴 질문 → 고성능 모델
            selected_model = main_model
            model_name = "Main (Qwen 14B)"
            max_tokens = 8192  # 고사양 최적화: 4096 → 8192 (n_ctx=16384의 50%)
            print(f"🤖 [Auto Mode] Question length={question_length} chars → Using Main Model (complex question)")

    else:  # thinking, research 등
        # Thinking/Research Mode: 무조건 고성능 모델
        selected_model = main_model
        model_name = "Main (Qwen 14B)"
        max_tokens = 8192  # 고사양 최적화: 4096 → 8192 (n_ctx=16384의 50%)

    # 모델 체크
    if not selected_model:
        return f"⚠️ {model_name} 모델 로딩 실패. 서버 로그 확인 요망."

    # 프롬프트 생성 (ChatML 형식)
    prompt = f"<|im_start|>system\n{system_prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>user\n{user_prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"

    try:
        print(f"🤖 [Hybrid Engine] Using {model_name} for mode='{mode}'")
        output = selected_model(
            prompt,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            stop=["<|im_end|>"],
            echo=False,
            temperature=0.7
        )
        return output['choices'][0]['text'].strip()
    except Exception as e:
        return f"⚠️ 생성 에러 ({model_name}): {str(e)}"

async def hybrid_generate_stream_async(system_prompt: str, user_prompt: str, mode: str = "thinking", question_text: str = "", conversation_context: str = "") -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:
    """
    Hybrid Dual Model Generation Engine - ASYNC STREAMING VERSION with GPU Lock

    Uses asyncio.Lock to ensure only one inference runs at a time.
    Other requests queue and wait without blocking the event loop.

    Args:
        conversation_context: 이전 대화 요약 (웹 검색 쿼리 개선용)

    Yields:
        Text chunks in real-time
    """
    # Acquire GPU lock before any inference
    logger.info(f"[GPU Queue] Waiting for lock... (mode={mode})")

    async with gpu_inference_lock:
        logger.info(f"[GPU Queue] Lock acquired, starting inference (mode={mode})")

        # Yield chunks from sync generator
        for chunk in _hybrid_generate_stream_sync(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode, question_text, conversation_context):
            yield chunk

        logger.info("[GPU Queue] Lock released")


def _hybrid_generate_stream_sync(system_prompt: str, user_prompt: str, mode: str = "thinking", question_text: str = "", conversation_context: str = "") -> Generator[str, None, None]:
    """
    Internal sync generator for streaming inference.
    Should only be called when GPU lock is held.

    Args:
        conversation_context: 이전 대화 요약 (웹 검색 쿼리 개선용)
    """
    # ========================================
    # [RESEARCH MODE] 웹 검색 결과 주입 (고사양 최적화 + 문맥 인식)
    # ========================================
    if mode == "research":
        if WEB_SEARCH_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                # ✅ [수정] 짧은 질문이면 대화 문맥을 포함한 검색어 생성
                search_query = question_text if question_text else user_prompt

                # 질문이 짧고 애매할 경우 대화 문맥 추가
                if len(search_query) < 20 and conversation_context:
                    # 이전 대화에서 키워드 추출 (간단한 방법: 마지막 문장)
                    context_lines = conversation_context.strip().split('\n')
                    if context_lines:
                        last_context = context_lines[-1]
                        search_query = f"{last_context} {search_query}"
                        print(f"📝 [Context-Aware Web Search] Enhanced query: '{search_query[:150]}...'")

                print(f"🌐 [Research Mode] Performing web search for: '{search_query[:100]}...'")
                web_results = search_web(search_query, max_results=5)

                # 토큰 제한 관리: 웹 검색 결과가 너무 길면 자르기
                web_results_truncated = truncate_to_token_limit(web_results, max_chars=12000)

                if len(web_results) != len(web_results_truncated):
                    print(f"⚠️ [Research Mode] Web results truncated: {len(web_results)} → {len(web_results_truncated)} chars")

                # 웹 검색 결과를 프롬프트에 명확하게 주입
                enhanced_user_prompt = f"""=== Web Search Results ===
{web_results_truncated}

=== User Question ===
{user_prompt}

=== Instruction ===
Answer the user's question by combining:
1. The web search results above (for up-to-date, external information)
2. Your own knowledge and reasoning

Provide a comprehensive, well-structured answer."""

                user_prompt = enhanced_user_prompt
                print(f"✅ [Research Mode] Web results injected into prompt (total length: {len(user_prompt)} chars)")

            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Research mode web search failed: {e}")
                user_prompt = f"[Note: Web search failed] {user_prompt}"
        else:
            logger.warning("⚠️ Research mode requested but web search not available")
            user_prompt = f"[Note: Web search unavailable] {user_prompt}"

    # ========================================
    # 모델 선택 (Intelligent Auto Mode) - 고사양 최적화
    # ========================================
    if mode == "fast":
        selected_model = fast_model
        model_name = "Fast (Qwen 3B)"
        max_tokens = 2048

    elif mode == "auto":
        # ✅ [개선] 스마트 모델 선택 (키워드 + 길이 + 감탄사 고려)
        actual_question = question_text if question_text else user_prompt
        question_length = len(actual_question)

        # should_use_main_model() 함수로 판단
        if should_use_main_model(actual_question):
            selected_model = main_model
            model_name = "Main (Qwen 14B)"
            max_tokens = 8192
            print(f"🤖 [Auto Mode] Question: '{actual_question[:50]}...' (len={question_length}) → Main Model")
        else:
            selected_model = fast_model
            model_name = "Fast (Qwen 3B)"
            max_tokens = 2048
            print(f"⚡ [Auto Mode] Question: '{actual_question[:50]}...' (len={question_length}) → Fast Model")

    else:  # thinking, research 등
        selected_model = main_model
        model_name = "Main (Qwen 14B)"
        max_tokens = 8192

    # 모델 체크
    if not selected_model:
        yield f"⚠️ {model_name} 모델 로딩 실패. 서버 로그 확인 요망."
        return

    # 프롬프트 생성 (ChatML 형식)
    prompt = f"<|im_start|>system\n{system_prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>user\n{user_prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"

    try:
        print(f"🤖 [Hybrid Engine - STREAMING] Using {model_name} for mode='{mode}'")
        print(f"📝 [Hybrid Engine] System Prompt Length: {len(system_prompt)} chars")
        print(f"📝 [Hybrid Engine] System Prompt Preview: '{system_prompt[:150]}...'")

        # ✅ STREAMING: stream=True로 설정
        stream = selected_model(
            prompt,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            stop=["<|im_end|>"],
            echo=False,
            temperature=0.7,
            stream=True  # 🔥 스트리밍 활성화
        )

        # 청크 단위로 yield
        for chunk in stream:
            if 'choices' in chunk and len(chunk['choices']) > 0:
                delta = chunk['choices'][0].get('text', '')
                if delta:
                    yield delta

    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = f"⚠️ 생성 에러 ({model_name}): {str(e)}"
        logger.error(error_msg)
        yield error_msg

# 하위 호환성을 위한 래퍼
def hybrid_generate_stream(system_prompt: str, user_prompt: str, mode: str = "thinking", question_text: str = "", conversation_context: str = "") -> Generator[str, None, None]:
    """
    Backward-compatible sync wrapper.
    WARNING: This does NOT use GPU lock. Use hybrid_generate_stream_async for production.
    """
    return _hybrid_generate_stream_sync(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode, question_text, conversation_context)


def local_llm_generate(system_prompt, user_prompt):
    """Deprecated: Use hybrid_generate instead"""
    return hybrid_generate(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode="thinking")

# --- GPU Queue Status Endpoint ---
@router.get("/gpu-status")
async def get_gpu_queue_status():
    """
    Get current GPU inference queue status.

    Returns:
        - lock_held: Whether the GPU lock is currently held
        - queue_status: Waiting count, processing status, total processed
    """
    if MODEL_SERVICE_AVAILABLE:
        service = get_model_service()
        return service.get_status()
    else:
        return {
            "main_model_loaded": main_model is not None,
            "fast_model_loaded": fast_model is not None,
            "lock_locked": gpu_inference_lock.locked(),
            "queue_status": {
                "waiting": 0,
                "processing": gpu_inference_lock.locked(),
                "total_processed": 0
            }
        }


# --- Endpoints ---
@router.post("/ask")
async def ask_question(request: Request, body: ChatRequest, current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    스트리밍 응답 엔드포인트 - 모든 모드(Fast/Thinking/Research) 지원

    실시간으로 텍스트 청크를 전송하고, 마지막에 메타데이터 전송
    """
    import time
    start_time = time.time()  # 시간 측정 시작

    # 1. 세션 처리 (스트리밍 시작 전 준비)
    session = None
    folder = None

    if body.session_id:
        session = db.query(ChatSession).filter(ChatSession.id == body.session_id).first()
        if session: folder = session.folder

    if not session:
        if body.folder_id and current_user:
            folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(AgentFolder.id == body.folder_id).first()

        session = ChatSession(
            user_id=current_user.id if current_user else None,
            folder_id=body.folder_id if folder else None,
            title=auto_title(body.question)
        )
        db.add(session)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(session)

    # ✅ [DEBUG] 폴더 로딩 확인
    if folder:
        print(f"📁 [Session] Using Folder: '{folder.name}' (ID: {folder.id})")
        print(f"📝 [Session] Folder System Prompt: '{folder.system_prompt[:100]}...'")
    else:
        print(f"⚠️ [Session] No folder assigned (using default system prompt)")

    # 2. 스트리밍 Generator 함수 정의
    async def stream_response():
        """실시간 텍스트 스트리밍 + 최종 메타데이터 전송"""
        nonlocal start_time
        full_answer = ""
        state_str = "COMPLETED"
        rag_used = False

        try:
            # [P0] 연결 끊김 감지 체크
            if await request.is_disconnected():
                logger.info("🔌 Client disconnected before streaming started")
                return
            # ========================================
            # [MODE BRANCHING] Fast vs Thinking Mode
            # ========================================

            if body.mode == "fast":
                # ⚡ FAST MODE: State Machine, RAG, 메모리 전부 생략
                print(f"⚡ [FAST MODE - STREAMING] Bypassing State Machine and RAG")
                try:
                    # 최근 대화 히스토리만 로드 (최대 5개)
                    history = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.session_id == session.id).order_by(ChatMessage.timestamp.desc()).limit(5).all()
                    history_list = [{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in reversed(history)]

                    # ✅ [FIX] 폴더의 시스템 프롬프트 사용 (Thinking 모드와 동일)
                    sys_prompt = folder.system_prompt if folder else "You are a helpful AI assistant. Answer concisely and directly."
                    print(f"📝 [FAST MODE] Using System Prompt from Folder: '{sys_prompt[:80]}...'")

                    # 히스토리를 포함한 사용자 프롬프트 생성
                    user_prompt = body.question
                    if history_list:
                        history_context = "【Recent conversation】\n"
                        for msg in history_list[-3:]:  # 최근 3개만
                            history_context += f"{msg['role']}: {msg['content'][:100]}\n"
                        user_prompt = history_context + "\n" + user_prompt

                    # ✅ 스트리밍 LLM 호출 (GPU Lock으로 동시성 제어)
                    context_summary = "\n".join([f"{msg['role']}: {msg['content'][:100]}" for msg in history_list[-3:]]) if history_list else ""

                    # 🔒 GPU Lock 획득 후 스트리밍 (async generator 사용)
                    async for chunk in hybrid_generate_stream_async(sys_prompt, user_prompt, mode="fast", question_text=body.question, conversation_context=context_summary):
                        # [P0] 청크 전송 전 연결 상태 체크
                        if await request.is_disconnected():
                            logger.info("🔌 Client disconnected during Fast Mode streaming")
                            return

                        full_answer += chunk
                        # SSE 형식으로 청크 전송
                        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'chunk': chunk}, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"

                    # Fast Mode에서는 생각 과정 제거 (간결한 답변만 표시)
                    if "<think>" in full_answer and "</think>" in full_answer:
                        parts = full_answer.split("</think>")
                        full_answer = parts[1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else full_answer

                    state_str = "FAST_MODE"
                    rag_used = False

                except Exception as e:
                    error_msg = f"⚠️ Fast Mode 오류: {str(e)}"
                    logger.error(f"Fast Mode Error: {e}")
                    full_answer = error_msg
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'chunk': error_msg}, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"
                    state_str = "ERROR"

            else:
                # 🧠 THINKING MODE: State Machine 로직 (RAG + 메모리 + 심층 사고) - STREAMING
                print(f"🧠 [THINKING MODE - STREAMING] Running full State Machine pipeline")

                if STATE_MACHINE_AVAILABLE:
                    try:
                        # 히스토리 및 메모리 로드
                        history = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.session_id == session.id).order_by(ChatMessage.timestamp.desc()).limit(5).all()
                        history_list = [{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in reversed(history)]

                        user_memories = []
                        if current_user:
                            mem_objs = db.query(UserMemory).filter(UserMemory.user_id == current_user.id).order_by(UserMemory.created_at.desc()).limit(5).all()
                            user_memories = [m.content for m in mem_objs]

                        sys_prompt = folder.system_prompt if folder else "You are a helpful AI assistant."
                        print(f"📝 [THINKING MODE] Using System Prompt from Folder: '{sys_prompt[:80]}...'")

                        # [RAG 설정]
                        use_rag = False
                        target_collection = None

                        # (A) 폴더 RAG
                        if folder and folder.use_rag:
                            use_rag = True
                            target_collection = f"folder_{folder.id}"
                            print(f"✅ [RAG] Folder Mode: {folder.name} -> Target: {target_collection}")

                        # (B) 임시 RAG
                        elif body.temp_session_id:
                            use_rag = True
                            target_collection = f"temp_{body.temp_session_id}"
                            print(f"✅ [RAG] Temp Mode: Target: {target_collection}")

                        # State Machine 생성
                        sm = create_state_machine(
                            user_query=body.question,
                            folder_system_prompt=sys_prompt,
                            use_rag=use_rag,
                            rag_collection_name=target_collection,
                            session_history=history_list,
                            user_memories=user_memories
                        )

                        # [검색 함수 래퍼]
                        def real_rag_search(query, collection_name=None, k=3):
                            if not use_rag or not target_collection:
                                return []

                            print(f"🔍 [RAG Search] Query: '{query}' in '{target_collection}'")
                            try:
                                results = search_documents(query, target_collection, k)
                                print(f"📊 [RAG Result] Found {len(results)} docs")
                                return results
                            except Exception as e:
                                print(f"⚠️ [RAG Error] {e}")
                                return []

                        # [LLM 생성 함수 래퍼 - STREAMING VERSION]
                        # State Machine은 스트리밍을 직접 지원하지 않으므로,
                        # 전체 답변을 생성하되 청크 단위로 yield
                        def llm_wrapper_streaming(system_prompt, user_prompt):
                            """State Machine용 스트리밍 래퍼"""
                            collected = ""
                            for chunk in hybrid_generate_stream(system_prompt, user_prompt, mode=body.mode, question_text=body.question):
                                collected += chunk
                                # 청크를 즉시 yield (외부 generator로 전파)
                                nonlocal full_answer
                                full_answer += chunk
                                yield chunk
                            return collected

                        # State Machine은 일반 함수를 기대하므로, 스트리밍 래퍼를 동기 함수로 변환
                        def llm_wrapper(system_prompt, user_prompt):
                            result = ""
                            for chunk in llm_wrapper_streaming(system_prompt, user_prompt):
                                result += chunk
                                # 실시간 청크 전송
                                yield f"data: {json.dumps({'chunk': chunk}, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"
                            return result

                        sm.set_rag_search_fn(real_rag_search)
                        sm.set_llm_generate_fn(hybrid_generate)  # 일단 기본 함수 사용 (State Machine 호환성)

                        # ✅ [수정] 대화 히스토리 포함한 프롬프트 생성
                        final_prompt = body.question

                        # 1. 대화 히스토리 추가 (최근 5개)
                        if history_list:
                            history_context = "=== Recent Conversation History ===\n"
                            for msg in history_list[-5:]:  # 최근 5개 메시지
                                history_context += f"{msg['role'].upper()}: {msg['content'][:200]}\n"  # 각 메시지 최대 200자
                            final_prompt = history_context + f"\n=== Current Question ===\n{body.question}"

                        # 2. RAG 검색 결과 추가
                        if use_rag:
                            # ✅ [수정] 대화 문맥을 고려한 RAG 검색
                            # 짧은 질문이면 이전 대화를 참고하여 검색
                            search_query = body.question
                            if len(body.question) < 20 and history_list:
                                # 이전 대화의 마지막 질문 포함
                                last_user_msg = next((msg['content'] for msg in reversed(history_list) if msg['role'] == 'user'), '')
                                if last_user_msg:
                                    search_query = f"{last_user_msg} {body.question}"
                                    print(f"📝 [Context-Aware Search] Combined query: '{search_query[:100]}...'")

                            rag_results = real_rag_search(search_query, target_collection, k=3)
                            if rag_results:
                                rag_context = "\n\n".join([f"[문서 {i+1}] {doc['text'][:500]}" for i, doc in enumerate(rag_results)])
                                final_prompt = f"=== 참고 문서 ===\n{rag_context}\n\n{final_prompt}"
                                rag_used = True

                        # 3. 사용자 메모리 추가 (있으면)
                        if user_memories:
                            memory_context = "\n=== User Memories ===\n" + "\n".join([f"- {mem}" for mem in user_memories[:3]])
                            final_prompt = memory_context + "\n\n" + final_prompt

                        # ✅ 스트리밍 생성 (GPU Lock으로 동시성 제어)
                        context_summary = "\n".join([f"{msg['role']}: {msg['content'][:100]}" for msg in history_list[-3:]]) if history_list else ""

                        # 🔒 GPU Lock 획득 후 스트리밍 (async generator 사용)
                        async for chunk in hybrid_generate_stream_async(sys_prompt, final_prompt, mode=body.mode, question_text=body.question, conversation_context=context_summary):
                            # [P0] 청크 전송 전 연결 상태 체크
                            if await request.is_disconnected():
                                logger.info("🔌 Client disconnected during Thinking Mode streaming")
                                return

                            full_answer += chunk
                            yield f"data: {json.dumps({'chunk': chunk}, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"

                        state_str = "STREAMING_COMPLETED"

                    except Exception as e:
                        error_msg = f"⚠️ 시스템 오류: {str(e)}"
                        logger.error(f"State Machine Error: {e}")
                        full_answer = error_msg
                        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'chunk': error_msg}, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"
                        state_str = "ERROR"
                else:
                    error_msg = "State Machine 모듈 로드 실패"
                    full_answer = error_msg
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'chunk': error_msg}, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"
                    state_str = "ERROR"

            # ========================================
            # 스트리밍 완료 후 DB 저장 및 메타데이터 전송
            # ========================================

            # 응답 시간 계산
            elapsed_time = time.time() - start_time

            # Auto 모드인 경우 실제 선택된 모드 판단
            auto_selected = body.mode == "auto"
            if body.mode == "auto":
                # ✅ [개선] 스마트 모델 선택과 동일한 로직 적용
                selected_mode = "thinking" if should_use_main_model(body.question) else "fast"
            else:
                selected_mode = body.mode

            # ✅ [FIX] 사용자 메시지 저장 (skip_user_message=True면 저장 안 함)
            if not body.skip_user_message:
                db.add(ChatMessage(session_id=session.id, role="user", content=body.question))

            # 어시스턴트 메시지 저장 (전체 답변)
            assistant_msg = ChatMessage(
                session_id=session.id,
                role="assistant",
                content=full_answer,
                state_info=state_str,
                rag_used=rag_used,
                auto_selected=auto_selected,
                selected_mode=selected_mode,
                processing_time=round(elapsed_time, 2)
            )
            db.add(assistant_msg)
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(assistant_msg)

            # 최종 메타데이터 전송 (SSE 종료 신호)
            metadata = {
                "done": True,
                "session_id": session.id,
                "message_id": assistant_msg.id,
                "processing_time": round(elapsed_time, 2),
                "rag_used": rag_used,
                "auto_selected": auto_selected,
                "selected_mode": selected_mode,
                "state": state_str
            }
            yield f"data: {json.dumps(metadata, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"

        except Exception as e:
            # 예기치 않은 에러 처리
            logger.error(f"Streaming error: {e}")
            error_data = {
                "error": True,
                "message": f"스트리밍 오류: {str(e)}"
            }
            yield f"data: {json.dumps(error_data, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"

    # 3. StreamingResponse 반환
    return StreamingResponse(
        stream_response(),
        media_type="text/event-stream",
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
            "Connection": "keep-alive",
            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no"  # Nginx 버퍼링 비활성화
        }
    )

# TTL Manager import
try:
    from ttl_manager import register_temp_resource, mark_resource_ready
    TTL_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TTL_AVAILABLE = False

# Event Logger import
try:
    from event_logger import log_event
    EVENT_LOGGER_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    EVENT_LOGGER_AVAILABLE = False

# [NEW] 임시 파일 업로드 API
@router.post("/upload/temp")
async def upload_temp_file(session_id: Optional[str] = Form(None), file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    if not session_id or session_id == "null":
        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    temp_dir = "data/uploads/temp"
    os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)

    file_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, f"{session_id}_{file.filename}")

    # [자원 식별 규칙] 컬렉션명은 반드시 temp_{session_id} 형식
    collection_name = f"temp_{session_id}"

    try:
        # [레이스 방지] 업로드 시작 시 UPLOADING 상태로 등록
        if TTL_AVAILABLE:
            register_temp_resource(
                temp_session_id=session_id,
                file_path=file_path,
                collection_name=collection_name,
                status="UPLOADING"  # 업로드 중 상태
            )
            logger.info(f"📝 [TTL] Registered UPLOADING resource: {session_id}")

        # 파일 저장
        with open(file_path, "wb") as buffer:
            shutil.copyfileobj(file.file, buffer)

        # 인덱싱
        print(f"📥 Indexing Temp: {file.filename} -> {collection_name}")
        result = process_and_index(file_path, collection_name)

        # [레이스 방지] 업로드 완료 시 READY 상태로 변경
        if TTL_AVAILABLE:
            mark_resource_ready(session_id)
            logger.info(f"✅ [TTL] Marked READY: {session_id}")

        return {
            "session_id": session_id,
            "filename": file.filename,
            "chunks": result['chunks']
        }

    except Exception as e:
        # 업로드 실패 시 리소스 정리 (선택적)
        logger.error(f"❌ [Upload] Failed: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(500, detail=str(e))

# --- Feedback & Delete APIs ---

@router.post("/messages/{message_id}/feedback")
def submit_feedback(message_id: int, body: FeedbackRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional)):
    """메시지 피드백"""
    # [SBMA][INTENT] thumbs_up/down 이벤트 로깅 (repeat/severity 산출 신호) - thumbs_down일 때 intent="thumbs_down" 로 기록
    message = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.id == message_id).first()
    if not message: raise HTTPException(404, detail="Message not found")
    
    feedback = db.query(MessageFeedback).filter(MessageFeedback.message_id == message_id).first()
    if feedback:
        feedback.is_positive = body.is_positive
        feedback.comment = body.comment
    else:
        feedback = MessageFeedback(message_id=message_id, is_positive=body.is_positive, comment=body.comment)
        db.add(feedback)
    
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}

@router.delete("/messages/{message_id}")
def delete_message(message_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional)):
    """메시지 삭제 (재생성용)"""
    # [SBMA][INTENT] delete_message는 edit/regenerate 파이프라인 신호로 취급 가능
    message = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.id == message_id).first()
    if not message: raise HTTPException(404)
    db.delete(message)
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}

class MessageUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    content: str
    processing_time: Optional[float] = None

@router.put("/messages/{message_id}")
def update_message(message_id: int, body: MessageUpdateRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional)):
    """메시지 내용 업데이트 (Continue용)"""
    message = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.id == message_id).first()
    if not message:
        raise HTTPException(404, detail="Message not found")

    # 내용 업데이트
    message.content = body.content
    if body.processing_time is not None:
        message.processing_time = body.processing_time

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(message)
    return {"ok": True, "message_id": message.id}

# --- Basic Session APIs ---

@router.post("/sessions", response_model=SessionResponse)
def create_session(body: SessionCreate, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    sess = ChatSession(user_id=current_user.id, folder_id=body.folder_id, title=body.title or "새 대화")
    db.add(sess)
    db.commit()
    return sess

@router.get("/sessions/uncategorized", response_model=List[SessionResponse])
def get_uncategorized(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    return db.query(ChatSession).filter(ChatSession.user_id==current_user.id, ChatSession.folder_id==None, ChatSession.is_active==True).order_by(ChatSession.created_at.desc()).all()

@router.get("/sessions/{session_id}/messages", response_model=List[MessageResponse])
def get_msgs(session_id: int, current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    # 메시지와 피드백 상태를 함께 반환
    msgs = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.session_id==session_id).order_by(ChatMessage.timestamp).all()
    result = []
    for m in msgs:
        fb = db.query(MessageFeedback).filter(MessageFeedback.message_id == m.id).first()
        res = MessageResponse.model_validate(m)
        res.feedback_positive = fb.is_positive if fb else None
        result.append(res)
    return result

@router.delete("/sessions/{session_id}")
def del_sess(session_id: int, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    sess = db.query(ChatSession).filter(ChatSession.id==session_id, ChatSession.user_id==current_user.id).first()
    if sess:
        sess.is_active = False
        db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}


@router.post("/sessions/{session_id}/restore_messages")
def restore_messages(
    session_id: int,
    body: RestoreMessagesRequest,
    current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Restore chat messages from a backup.

    This endpoint deletes all existing messages for the session and bulk inserts
    the provided messages. Used for "Undo" functionality in the frontend.
    """
    # Verify session exists
    session = db.query(ChatSession).filter(ChatSession.id == session_id).first()
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")

    try:
        # Delete all existing messages for this session
        deleted_count = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(
            ChatMessage.session_id == session_id
        ).delete(synchronize_session=False)

        logger.info(f"🗑️ [Restore] Deleted {deleted_count} existing messages from session {session_id}")

        # Bulk insert new messages
        new_messages = []
        for msg_item in body.messages:
            # Parse timestamp if provided, otherwise use current time
            msg_timestamp = None
            if msg_item.timestamp:
                try:
                    msg_timestamp = datetime.fromisoformat(msg_item.timestamp.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
                except ValueError:
                    msg_timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
            else:
                msg_timestamp = datetime.utcnow()

            new_msg = ChatMessage(
                session_id=session_id,
                role=msg_item.role,
                content=msg_item.content,
                rag_used=msg_item.rag_used,
                auto_selected=msg_item.auto_selected,
                selected_mode=msg_item.selected_mode,
                processing_time=msg_item.processing_time,
                timestamp=msg_timestamp
            )
            new_messages.append(new_msg)

        # Add all messages to the session
        db.add_all(new_messages)
        db.commit()

        logger.info(f"✅ [Restore] Restored {len(new_messages)} messages to session {session_id}")

        return {
            "ok": True,
            "restored_count": len(new_messages),
            "session_id": session_id
        }

    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        logger.error(f"❌ [Restore] Failed to restore messages for session {session_id}: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to restore messages: {str(e)}")


# --- Developer Report API (P1) ---

class DeveloperReportRequest(BaseModel):
    category: str  # "bug", "request", "other"
    summary: str
    details: str
    session_id: Optional[int] = None
    message_id: Optional[int] = None


@router.post("/report")
def submit_developer_report(
    body: DeveloperReportRequest,
    current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    개발자 리포트 제출 (P1)

    유저가 버그/요청/피드백을 즉시 제출할 수 있는 엔드포인트
    자동으로 현재 세션 ID 및 마지막 AI 응답 메타데이터를 첨부
    """
    try:
        # 컨텍스트 메타데이터 수집
        context_metadata = {
            "category": body.category,
            "summary": body.summary,
            "user_id": current_user.id if current_user else None,
            "username": current_user.username if current_user else "guest"
        }

        # 세션 정보 추가
        if body.session_id:
            session = db.query(ChatSession).filter(ChatSession.id == body.session_id).first()
            if session:
                context_metadata["session_title"] = session.title
                context_metadata["session_created_at"] = session.created_at.isoformat()

                # 마지막 AI 응답 정보 추가
                last_ai_message = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(
                    ChatMessage.session_id == body.session_id,
                    ChatMessage.role == "assistant"
                ).order_by(ChatMessage.timestamp.desc()).first()

                if last_ai_message:
                    context_metadata["last_ai_message_id"] = last_ai_message.id
                    context_metadata["last_ai_message_preview"] = last_ai_message.content[:200]
                    context_metadata["last_ai_mode"] = last_ai_message.selected_mode
                    context_metadata["last_ai_processing_time"] = last_ai_message.processing_time

        # 메시지 정보 추가
        if body.message_id:
            message = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(ChatMessage.id == body.message_id).first()
            if message:
                context_metadata["message_role"] = message.role
                context_metadata["message_preview"] = message.content[:200]

        # 이벤트 로깅
        if EVENT_LOGGER_AVAILABLE:
            log_event(
                event_type=body.category,
                message=f"[{body.summary}] {body.details}",
                session_id=body.session_id,
                message_id=body.message_id,
                user_id=current_user.id if current_user else None,
                context_metadata=context_metadata
            )
            logger.info(f"✅ [Report] Submitted by {current_user.username if current_user else 'guest'}: {body.category} - {body.summary}")
        else:
            logger.warning("⚠️ Event Logger not available, report not logged")

        return {
            "ok": True,
            "message": "리포트가 성공적으로 제출되었습니다.",
            "event_type": body.category
        }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ [Report] Failed to submit report: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

# File name: config.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# config.py - Configuration management using Pydantic BaseSettings
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import List
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """Application settings loaded from environment variables and .env file."""

    # API Key for authentication
    api_key: str = "your-secure-api-key-here"

    # Server Configuration
    server_host: str = "0.0.0.0"
    server_port: int = 8002

    # Application Settings
    app_title: str = "Aventurine v3"
    debug: bool = False

    # CORS Settings
    cors_origins: str = "*"

    # RBAC: Daily Usage Limits
    limit_admin: int = -1  # -1 means unlimited
    limit_user: int = 150
    limit_guest: int = 20

    # Guest Access Code (for guest login)
    guest_access_code: str = "guest-access-code-here"

    # Guest Token Expiry (hours)
    guest_token_expire_hours: int = 24

    class Config:
        env_file = ".env"
        env_file_encoding = "utf-8"
        case_sensitive = False

    def get_cors_origins(self) -> List[str]:
        """Parse CORS origins from comma-separated string."""
        if self.cors_origins == "*":
            return ["*"]
        return [origin.strip() for origin in self.cors_origins.split(",")]

    def get_limit_for_role(self, role: str) -> int:
        """Get daily request limit for a given role."""
        limits = {
            "admin": self.limit_admin,
            "user": self.limit_user,
            "guest": self.limit_guest,
        }
        return limits.get(role, self.limit_guest)


@lru_cache()
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    """Get cached settings instance."""
    return Settings()

# File name: database.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# database.py — Aventurine v3: RBAC and Daily Usage Limits
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, date
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, Column, Integer, String, Text, DateTime, Boolean, ForeignKey, Float, Date, UniqueConstraint, Enum as SQLEnum
from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, relationship, Session
from pathlib import Path
import os
import enum
from typing import Optional

from passlib.context import CryptContext
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from jose import jwt, JWTError
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status, Header


# =========================
# Role Enum
# =========================

class UserRole(str, enum.Enum):
    """User roles for RBAC."""
    ADMIN = "admin"
    USER = "user"
    GUEST = "guest"

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
DATA_DIR = Path(os.getenv("AI_LOCAL_DATA", BASE_DIR / "data"))
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "aventurine_v3.db"
DB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# SQLite 설정
DATABASE_URL = f"sqlite:///{DB_PATH}"
engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False})
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)
Base = declarative_base()

# Auth 설정
SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "aventurine-v3-secret")
ALGORITHM = "HS256"
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 60 * 24 * 7

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
# auto_error=False: 토큰이 없어도 401 에러를 바로 뱉지 않음 (게스트 허용)
oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/auth/token", auto_error=False)


# =========================
# Models
