class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    username = Column(String(255), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    hashed_password = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    role = Column(String(20), default=UserRole.USER.value, nullable=False)  # RBAC: admin, user, guest
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    # Relationships
    folders = relationship("AgentFolder", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    sessions = relationship("ChatSession", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    memories = relationship("UserMemory", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    daily_usages = relationship("DailyUsage", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class AgentFolder(Base):
    __tablename__ = "agent_folders"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    system_prompt = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    use_rag = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    rag_collection_name = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    icon = Column(String(10), default="📁", nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    is_creative = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)  # 소설/창작 모드 (재생성 허용)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="folders")
    sessions = relationship("ChatSession", back_populates="folder")
    documents = relationship("FolderDocument", back_populates="folder", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

# [SBMA][LONG] UserMemory.content는 IF-THEN 규칙만 저장 (서사/성격/날짜/고유명사 금지)
class UserMemory(Base):
    __tablename__ = "user_memories"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="memories")


class ChatSession(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chat_sessions"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True, index=True)  # v3: nullable (게스트)
    folder_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("agent_folders.id"), nullable=True, index=True)  # v3: nullable (분류 없음)
    title = Column(String(500), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="sessions")
    folder = relationship("AgentFolder", back_populates="sessions")
    messages = relationship("ChatMessage", back_populates="session", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class ChatMessage(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chat_messages"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    session_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chat_sessions.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    role = Column(String(50), nullable=False)
    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    state_info = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    rag_used = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    timestamp = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    auto_selected = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)  # Auto 모드 사용 여부
    selected_mode = Column(String(50), nullable=True)  # 실제 사용된 모드 (fast/thinking/research)
    processing_time = Column(Float, nullable=True)  # 응답 생성 시간 (초)

    session = relationship("ChatSession", back_populates="messages")


class FolderDocument(Base):
    """폴더에 업로드된 문서 메타데이터 (영구 RAG용)"""
    __tablename__ = "folder_documents"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    folder_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("agent_folders.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    filename = Column(String(500), nullable=False)
    file_path = Column(String(1000), nullable=False)
    uploaded_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    folder = relationship("AgentFolder", back_populates="documents")


class MessageFeedback(Base):
    """메시지 피드백 (좋아요/싫어요)"""
    __tablename__ = "message_feedbacks"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    message_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chat_messages.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    is_positive = Column(Boolean, nullable=False)  # True=좋아요, False=싫어요
    comment = Column(Text, nullable=True)  # 선택적 코멘트
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    message = relationship("ChatMessage")


class TempResource(Base):
    """임시 리소스 추적 (TTL 기반 자동 정리용)"""
    __tablename__ = "temp_resources"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    temp_session_id = Column(String(255), nullable=False, index=True)  # 임시 세션 ID (UUID)
    file_path = Column(String(1000), nullable=True)  # 파일 경로 (data/uploads/temp/...)
    collection_name = Column(String(255), nullable=True)  # ChromaDB 컬렉션 이름 (temp_xxx)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)  # 생성 시각
    status = Column(String(50), default="UPLOADING", nullable=False)  # UPLOADING, READY, EXPIRE_FAILED, EXPIRED
    retry_count = Column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)  # 재시도 횟수

    # TTL: 30분 (1800초) - READY 기준
    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        return (datetime.utcnow() - self.created_at).total_seconds() > 1800

    # UPLOADING 고아 감지: 2시간 (7200초)
    @property
    def is_uploading_orphan(self):
        return self.status == "UPLOADING" and (datetime.utcnow() - self.created_at).total_seconds() > 7200


class EventLog(Base):
    """단기 기억: 유저 피드백/버그/요청 이벤트 로그 (JSONL 백업용)"""
    __tablename__ = "event_logs"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    event_type = Column(String(50), nullable=False)  # feedback, bug, request, error
    session_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chat_sessions.id"), nullable=True, index=True)
    message_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chat_messages.id"), nullable=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True, index=True)
    message = Column(Text, nullable=False)  # 원문 메시지
    context_metadata = Column(Text, nullable=True)  # JSON 직렬화된 메타데이터
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    processed = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)  # 장기 기억으로 승격 여부


class DailyUsage(Base):
    """Daily usage tracking for rate limiting per user."""
    __tablename__ = "daily_usages"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    date = Column(Date, nullable=False, index=True)
    count = Column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)

    # Unique constraint: one record per user per day
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint('user_id', 'date', name='uq_user_date'),
    )

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="daily_usages")


# =========================

def init_db():
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()


def create_default_folders_for_user(db: Session, user_id: int):
    """회원가입 시 기본 폴더 생성"""
    default_folders = [
        {
            "name": "일반 대화",
            "icon": "💬",
            "system_prompt": "너는 친절한 AI 어시스턴트다.",
            "use_rag": False,
            "rag_collection_name": None
        },
        {
            "name": "SQL 전문가",
            "icon": "🗄️",
            "system_prompt": "너는 SQL 전문가다. 쿼리를 작성하고 설명한다.",
            "use_rag": True,
            "rag_collection_name": f"user_{user_id}_sql"
        },
        {
            "name": "문서 검색",
            "icon": "📚",
            "system_prompt": "너는 문서 전문가다. 검색하여 정보를 제공한다.",
            "use_rag": True,
            "rag_collection_name": f"user_{user_id}_docs"
        }
    ]

    for folder_data in default_folders:
        folder = AgentFolder(user_id=user_id, **folder_data)
        db.add(folder)

    db.commit()


# =========================
# Auth Functions
# =========================

def verify_password(plain_password, hashed_password):
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)


def get_password_hash(password):
    return pwd_context.hash(password)


def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: timedelta = None, role: str = UserRole.USER.value):
    """Create JWT token with role included in payload."""
    to_encode = data.copy()
    if expires_delta:
        expire = datetime.utcnow() + expires_delta
    else:
        expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=15)
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire, "role": role})
    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)
    return encoded_jwt


def get_current_user_optional(
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
) -> Optional[User]:
    """
    게스트 모드 지원: 토큰이 있으면 유저 반환, 없거나 틀리면 None 반환

    [Security] Cancel/Abort 시 인증되지 않은 접근 방지:
    - 토큰이 없으면 None (정상적인 비인증 상태)
    - 토큰 검증 실패 시 HTTPException 발생 (비정상 접근 차단)
    """
    if not authorization:
        return None

    try:
        # "Bearer " 접두사 제거 처리
        token = authorization.replace("Bearer ", "")

        # 빈 토큰 검증 (Cancel/Abort로 인한 빈 문자열 방지)
        if not token or token.strip() == "":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
                detail="Empty or invalid token provided",
                headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
            )

        payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        username: str = payload.get("sub")

        if username is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
                detail="Token payload missing subject",
                headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
            )

    except JWTError as e:
        # 토큰 검증 실패 - 명시적 거부 (None 반환 대신)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=f"Token validation failed: {type(e).__name__}",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    except AttributeError:
        # 잘못된 토큰 형식
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Malformed authorization header",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == username).first()

    if user is None:
        # 토큰은 유효하지만 사용자가 없음 (삭제된 사용자 등)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="User not found for valid token",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    return user


def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """보안 라우트용: 인증 실패 시 401 에러 발생"""
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Not authenticated",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )

    if not token:
        raise credentials_exception

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        username: str = payload.get("sub")
        if username is None:
            raise credentials_exception
    except JWTError:
        raise credentials_exception

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == username).first()
    if user is None:
        raise credentials_exception
    return user


def get_or_create_guest_user(db: Session) -> User:
    """Get or create a guest user for guest login."""
    import uuid
    guest_username = f"guest_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"

    # Create guest user with a random hashed password (not usable for login)
    guest_user = User(
        username=guest_username,
        hashed_password=get_password_hash(uuid.uuid4().hex),
        role=UserRole.GUEST.value
    )
    db.add(guest_user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(guest_user)
    return guest_user


def get_token_payload(token: str) -> dict:
    """Decode token and return payload without validation (for role extraction)."""
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        return payload
    except JWTError:
        return {}


# DB 초기화 실행
init_db()

# File name: event_logger.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# event_logger.py — JSONL-based Event Logging System
"""
단기 기억 수집: 유저 피드백/버그/요청 이벤트 로그
- JSONL 형식으로 저장 (append-only, no modification)
- 필수 필드: type, timestamp, session_id, message, context_metadata
- DB와 파일 양쪽에 저장 (이중 백업)
"""

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Optional, Any
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from database import SessionLocal, EventLog

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Configuration
EVENTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "data" / "events"
EVENTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
EVENTS_FILE = EVENTS_DIR / "events.jsonl"

# [SBMA][INTENT]
# explicit intents: regenerate/edit/thumbs_down/report/abort/stop 등을 event_type/context_metadata로 표준화

# [SBMA][INTENT] intent 표준 키
SBMA_INTENTS = {
    "edit",
    "regenerate",
    "thumbs_up",
    "thumbs_down",
    "report",
    "abort",
    "stop",
    "delete_message",
}

def _merge_context_metadata(base: Optional[Dict[str, Any]], extra: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    b = base or {}
    e = extra or {}
    return {**b, **e}


def log_event(
    event_type: str,
    message: str,
    session_id: Optional[int] = None,
    message_id: Optional[int] = None,
    user_id: Optional[int] = None,
    context_metadata: Optional[Dict] = None
) -> bool:
    timestamp = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()

    # [SBMA] meta normalize
    meta: Dict[str, Any] = context_metadata or {}

    # intent면 meta.intent 강제
    if event_type == "intent":
        if "intent" not in meta or not meta["intent"]:
            meta["intent"] = "unknown"
        else:
            meta["intent"] = str(meta["intent"]).strip().lower()

    event_record = {
        "event_type": event_type,
        "timestamp": timestamp,
        "session_id": session_id,
        "message_id": message_id,
        "user_id": user_id,
        "message": message,
        "context_metadata": meta
    }

    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        with open(EVENTS_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(event_record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        logger.info(f"📝 [Event] Logged to JSONL: {event_type} - {message[:50]}...")

        event_log = EventLog(
            event_type=event_type,
            session_id=session_id,
            message_id=message_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            message=message,
            context_metadata=json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False),
            created_at=datetime.utcnow(),
            processed=False
        )
        db.add(event_log)
        db.commit()
        logger.info(f"📝 [Event] Logged to DB: {event_type} (ID: {event_log.id})")

        return True

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ [Event] Failed to log event: {e}")
        db.rollback()
        return False
    finally:
        db.close()

def log_intent(
    intent: str,
    message: str = "",
    session_id: Optional[int] = None,
    message_id: Optional[int] = None,
    user_id: Optional[int] = None,
    ui: Optional[str] = None,
    reason: Optional[str] = None,
    origin_message_id: Optional[int] = None,
    extra: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> bool:
    """
    [SBMA][INTENT] intent 통일 기록기
    - event_type은 항상 "intent"
    - context_metadata.intent는 표준 키로 강제
    """
    intent_norm = (intent or "").strip().lower()

    # 엄격하게 막고 싶으면 unknown 처리, 아니면 그대로 허용
    if intent_norm not in SBMA_INTENTS:
        intent_norm = intent_norm or "unknown"

    meta: Dict[str, Any] = {"intent": intent_norm}

    if ui:
        meta["ui"] = ui
    if reason:
        meta["reason"] = reason
    if origin_message_id is not None:
        meta["origin_message_id"] = origin_message_id

    if extra:
        meta = _merge_context_metadata(meta, extra)

    return log_event(
        event_type="intent",
        message=message,
        session_id=session_id,
        message_id=message_id,
        user_id=user_id,
        context_metadata=meta
    )


def get_unprocessed_events(limit: int = 100) -> list:
    """
    미처리 이벤트 조회 (장기 기억 승격용)

    Args:
        limit: 최대 조회 개수

    Returns:
        List of EventLog objects
    """
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        events = db.query(EventLog).filter(
            EventLog.processed == False
        ).order_by(EventLog.created_at.asc()).limit(limit).all()

        logger.info(f"📊 [Event] Found {len(events)} unprocessed events")
        return events
    finally:
        db.close()


def mark_event_processed(event_id: int) -> bool:
    """
    이벤트를 처리됨으로 표시

    Args:
        event_id: EventLog ID

    Returns:
        True if successful, False otherwise
    """
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        event = db.query(EventLog).filter(EventLog.id == event_id).first()
        if event:
            event.processed = True
            db.commit()
            logger.info(f"✅ [Event] Marked event {event_id} as processed")
            return True
        else:
            logger.warning(f"⚠️ [Event] Event {event_id} not found")
            return False
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ [Event] Failed to mark event {event_id}: {e}")
        db.rollback()
        return False
    finally:
        db.close()


def get_event_stats() -> Dict[str, int]:
    """
    이벤트 통계 조회

    Returns:
        Dict with stats: {
            "total": int,
            "processed": int,
            "unprocessed": int,
            "by_type": {...}
        }
    """
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        total = db.query(EventLog).count()
        processed = db.query(EventLog).filter(EventLog.processed == True).count()
        unprocessed = db.query(EventLog).filter(EventLog.processed == False).count()

        # 타입별 통계
        from sqlalchemy import func
        by_type = {}
        type_counts = db.query(
            EventLog.event_type,
            func.count(EventLog.id)
        ).group_by(EventLog.event_type).all()

        for event_type, count in type_counts:
            by_type[event_type] = count

        return {
            "total": total,
            "processed": processed,
            "unprocessed": unprocessed,
            "by_type": by_type
        }
    finally:
        db.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 테스트 실행
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    logger.info("🧪 Running Event Logger Test...")

    # 테스트 이벤트 로깅
    log_event(
        event_type="test",
        message="This is a test event",
        session_id=1,
        context_metadata={"test": True, "version": "v3"}
    )

    # 통계 조회
    stats = get_event_stats()
    print(f"Event Stats: {json.dumps(stats, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)}")

# events_api.py — Event Ingress API (단기 이벤트 수집 입구)

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, Dict
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from database import get_db
from event_logger import log_event

router = APIRouter(tags=["events"])


class EventIn(BaseModel):
    type: str
    message: str
    session_id: Optional[int] = None
    message_id: Optional[int] = None
    user_id: Optional[int] = None
    context_metadata: Optional[Dict] = None


@router.post("")
# [SBMA][INTENT] UI에서 abort/stop/edit/regenerate/thumbs_down 등을 이 엔드포인트로 전송
def ingest_event(event: EventIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    단기 이벤트 수집 엔드포인트
    - feedback / bug / request / debug / error 등
    - JSONL + DB 이중 저장
    """

    ok = log_event(
        event_type=event.type,
        message=event.message,
        session_id=event.session_id,
        message_id=event.message_id,
        user_id=event.user_id,
        context_metadata=event.context_metadata
    )

    if not ok:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to log event")

    return {"ok": True}

class FolderCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    system_prompt: Optional[str] = "너는 도움이 되는 AI 어시스턴트다."
    use_rag: bool = False
    rag_collection_name: Optional[str] = None
    icon: str = "📁"
    is_creative: bool = False  # 소설/창작 모드


class FolderUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    system_prompt: Optional[str] = None
    use_rag: Optional[bool] = None
    rag_collection_name: Optional[str] = None
    icon: Optional[str] = None
    is_creative: Optional[bool] = None


class FolderResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    name: str
    system_prompt: Optional[str]
    use_rag: bool
    rag_collection_name: Optional[str]
    icon: str
    created_at: datetime
    session_count: int = 0
    is_creative: bool = False

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

    id: int
    title: str
    created_at: datetime
    message_count: int = 0

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

@router.get("", response_model=List[FolderResponse])
def get_user_folders(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    # [수정] Depends를 사용하여 인증된 사용자를 가져옵니다.
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """사용자의 모든 폴더 조회"""
    folders = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id,
        AgentFolder.is_active == True
    ).order_by(AgentFolder.created_at.asc()).all()

    # [수정] SQLAlchemy 객체를 안전하게 변환
    result = []
    for folder in folders:
        session_count = db.query(ChatSession).filter(
            ChatSession.folder_id == folder.id,
            ChatSession.is_active == True
        ).count()
        
        # Pydantic 모델에 맞게 데이터 매핑
        folder_data = FolderResponse(
            id=folder.id,
            name=folder.name,
            system_prompt=folder.system_prompt,
            use_rag=folder.use_rag,
            rag_collection_name=folder.rag_collection_name,
            icon=folder.icon,
            created_at=folder.created_at,
            session_count=session_count,
            is_creative=folder.is_creative
        )
        result.append(folder_data)

    return result


@router.post("", response_model=FolderResponse)
def create_folder(
    body: FolderCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """새 폴더 생성"""
    # 기본 컬렉션 이름 자동 생성
    collection_name = body.rag_collection_name
    if body.use_rag and not collection_name:
        collection_name = f"user_{current_user.id}_docs"

    new_folder = AgentFolder(
        user_id=current_user.id,
        name=body.name,
        system_prompt=body.system_prompt,
        use_rag=body.use_rag,
        rag_collection_name=collection_name,
        icon=body.icon,
        is_active=True,
        is_creative=body.is_creative,
        created_at=datetime.utcnow() # 명시적 시간 설정
    )
    db.add(new_folder)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(new_folder)

    # 응답 생성
    return FolderResponse(
        id=new_folder.id,
        name=new_folder.name,
        system_prompt=new_folder.system_prompt,
        use_rag=new_folder.use_rag,
        rag_collection_name=new_folder.rag_collection_name,
        icon=new_folder.icon,
        created_at=new_folder.created_at,
        session_count=0,
        is_creative=new_folder.is_creative
    )


@router.patch("/{folder_id}", response_model=FolderResponse)
def update_folder(
    folder_id: int,
    body: FolderUpdate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """폴더 정보 수정"""
    folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.id == folder_id,
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()

    if not folder:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Folder not found")

    # 업데이트
    if body.name is not None:
        folder.name = body.name
    if body.system_prompt is not None:
        folder.system_prompt = body.system_prompt
    if body.use_rag is not None:
        folder.use_rag = body.use_rag
    if body.rag_collection_name is not None:
        folder.rag_collection_name = body.rag_collection_name
    if body.icon is not None:
        folder.icon = body.icon
    if body.is_creative is not None:
        folder.is_creative = body.is_creative

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(folder)

    session_count = db.query(ChatSession).filter(
        ChatSession.folder_id == folder.id,
        ChatSession.is_active == True
    ).count()

    return FolderResponse(
        id=folder.id,
        name=folder.name,
        system_prompt=folder.system_prompt,
        use_rag=folder.use_rag,
        rag_collection_name=folder.rag_collection_name,
        icon=folder.icon,
        created_at=folder.created_at,
        session_count=session_count,
        is_creative=folder.is_creative
    )


@router.delete("/{folder_id}")
def delete_folder(
    folder_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """폴더 삭제 (소프트 삭제)"""
    folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.id == folder_id,
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()

    if not folder:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Folder not found")

    folder.is_active = False
    db.commit()

    return {"ok": True}


@router.get("/{folder_id}/sessions", response_model=List[SessionInFolderResponse])
def get_folder_sessions(
    folder_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """특정 폴더의 모든 세션 조회"""
    # 폴더 소유권 확인
    folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.id == folder_id,
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()

    if not folder:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Folder not found")

    sessions = db.query(ChatSession).filter(
        ChatSession.folder_id == folder_id,
        ChatSession.is_active == True
    ).order_by(ChatSession.created_at.desc()).all()

    result = []
    for session in sessions:
        message_count = db.query(ChatMessage).filter(
            ChatMessage.session_id == session.id
        ).count()

        result.append(SessionInFolderResponse(
            id=session.id,
            title=session.title,
            created_at=session.created_at,
            message_count=message_count
        ))

    return result


# =========================
# RAG File Upload Endpoints
# =========================

class DocumentUploadResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    filename: str
    chunks: int
    message: str
    document_id: Optional[int] = None

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True


class DocumentListResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    filename: str
    uploaded_at: datetime

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True


@router.post("/{folder_id}/upload", response_model=DocumentUploadResponse)
async def upload_document_to_folder(
    folder_id: int,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """
    폴더에 문서 업로드 및 RAG 인덱싱 (영구 저장)

    - 파일을 data/uploads/{folder_id}/ 에 저장
    - ChromaDB의 folder_{folder_id} 컬렉션에 인덱싱
    - FolderDocument 테이블에 메타데이터 저장
    """
    if not RAG_AVAILABLE:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="RAG module not available")

    # 1. 폴더 소유권 확인
    folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.id == folder_id,
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()

    if not folder:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Folder not found")

    # 2. 파일 저장
    try:
        # 업로드 디렉토리 생성
        upload_dir = ensure_upload_dir(str(folder_id))
        file_path = upload_dir / file.filename

        # 파일 쓰기 (비동기)
        async with aiofiles.open(file_path, 'wb') as out_file:
            content = await file.read()
            await out_file.write(content)

    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"File save error: {str(e)}")

    # 3. RAG 인덱싱
    collection_name = f"folder_{folder_id}"
    result = process_and_index(str(file_path), collection_name)

    if not result["success"]:
        # 실패 시 파일 삭제
        file_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=result["message"])

    # 4. DB에 메타데이터 저장
    doc_record = FolderDocument(
        folder_id=folder_id,
        filename=file.filename,
        file_path=str(file_path)
    )
    db.add(doc_record)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(doc_record)

    # 5. 폴더의 RAG 설정 자동 활성화 (선택사항)
    if not folder.use_rag:
        folder.use_rag = True
        folder.rag_collection_name = collection_name
        db.commit()

    return DocumentUploadResponse(
        success=True,
        filename=file.filename,
        chunks=result["chunks"],
        message=result["message"],
        document_id=doc_record.id
    )


@router.get("/{folder_id}/documents", response_model=List[DocumentListResponse])
def get_folder_documents(
    folder_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """폴더에 업로드된 문서 목록 조회"""
    # 폴더 소유권 확인
    folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.id == folder_id,
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()

    if not folder:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Folder not found")

    # 문서 목록 조회
    documents = db.query(FolderDocument).filter(
        FolderDocument.folder_id == folder_id
    ).order_by(FolderDocument.uploaded_at.desc()).all()

    return [
        DocumentListResponse(
            id=doc.id,
            filename=doc.filename,
            uploaded_at=doc.uploaded_at
        )
        for doc in documents
    ]


@router.delete("/{folder_id}/documents/{document_id}")
def delete_folder_document(
    folder_id: int,
    document_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(database.get_current_user)
):
    """폴더의 문서 삭제 (파일 + DB 메타데이터)"""
    # 폴더 소유권 확인
    folder = db.query(AgentFolder).filter(
        AgentFolder.id == folder_id,
        AgentFolder.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()

    if not folder:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Folder not found")

    # 문서 조회
    document = db.query(FolderDocument).filter(
        FolderDocument.id == document_id,
        FolderDocument.folder_id == folder_id
    ).first()

    if not document:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Document not found")

    # 파일 삭제
    try:
        file_path = Path(document.file_path)
        if file_path.exists():
            file_path.unlink()
    except Exception as e:
        # 파일 삭제 실패해도 DB 레코드는 삭제
        pass

    # DB 레코드 삭제
    db.delete(document)
    db.commit()

    return {"ok": True, "message": "Document deleted"}

    TTL_AVAILABLE = True

    TTL_AVAILABLE = False

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title="Aventurine v3")

# [P0] 백그라운드 TTL 스윕 태스크
async def background_ttl_sweeper():
    """5분 주기로 만료된 임시 리소스 정리"""
    logger.info("🧹 [Background] TTL Sweeper started")
    while True:
        try:
            await asyncio.sleep(SWEEP_INTERVAL_SECONDS)  # 5분 대기

            # 만료된 리소스 스윕
            stats = sweep_expired_resources()
            if stats["resources_expired"] > 0:
                logger.info(f"🧹 [Background] TTL Sweep completed: {stats}")

            # 고아 파일 정리
            orphan_count = cleanup_orphaned_temp_files()
            if orphan_count > 0:
                logger.info(f"🧹 [Background] Orphan cleanup: {orphan_count} files removed")

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ [Background] TTL Sweeper error: {e}", exc_info=True)



# 루트 경로('/')에 대한 배팅 구역 생성
@app.get("/")
async def root():
    return {"message": "Aventurine System Online. 준비됐나, 친구?"}

# 서버 시작 시 파일-DB 동기화 실행 + TTL 스윕 시작
@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_event():
    """서버 시작 시 ChromaDB와 파일 시스템 동기화 + TTL 스윕 시작"""
    # 1. RAG 동기화
    if RAG_SYNC_AVAILABLE:
        try:
            print("\n" + "="*60)
            print("🔄 Starting ChromaDB-File Synchronization...")
            print("="*60)
            result = sync_db_with_files()
            total = result.get("total_removed", 0)
            if total > 0:
                print(f"✅ Removed {total} orphaned documents from ChromaDB")
            else:
                print("✅ All database entries are in sync with files")
            print("="*60 + "\n")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Warning: DB sync failed: {e}")
    else:
        print("⚠️ RAG sync not available, skipping DB synchronization")

    # 2. TTL 백그라운드 스윕 시작
    if TTL_AVAILABLE:
        print("\n" + "="*60)
        print("🧹 Starting TTL Background Sweeper...")
        print(f"   - Sweep Interval: {SWEEP_INTERVAL_SECONDS} seconds (5 minutes)")
        print(f"   - TTL Policy: 1800 seconds (30 minutes)")
        print("="*60 + "\n")
        asyncio.create_task(background_ttl_sweeper())
    else:
        print("⚠️ TTL Manager not available, skipping background sweeper")
        # [SBMA][BATCH]

# - daily: memory_pipeline.sbma_cleanup_short_events()

# 4. 회원가입용 데이터 모델 (JSON 처리용)
class RegisterRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str

# 5. 인증 엔드포인트
@app.post("/auth/register")
def register(user_data: RegisterRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    if db.query(User).filter(User.username == user_data.username).first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="User exists")
    
    user = User(username=user_data.username, hashed_password=get_password_hash(user_data.password))
    db.add(user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)
    
    create_default_folders_for_user(db, user.id)

    access_token = create_access_token(
        {"sub": user.username},
        expires_delta=timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES),
    )
    return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer"}

@app.post("/auth/token")
def login(form: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == form.username).first()
    if not user or not verify_password(form.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Bad credentials")

    access_token = create_access_token(
        {"sub": user.username},
        expires_delta=timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES),
    )
    return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer"}


@app.get("/auth/me")
def me(u: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return {"username": u.username, "id": u.id}

# 수동 DB 동기화 엔드포인트 (관리용)
@app.post("/admin/sync-db")
def manual_db_sync(u: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    """수동으로 ChromaDB-파일 동기화 실행 (관리자용)"""
    if not RAG_SYNC_AVAILABLE:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="RAG sync not available")

    try:
        result = sync_db_with_files()
        return {
            "success": True,
            "total_removed": result.get("total_removed", 0),
            "collections": result.get("collections", {}),
            "message": f"Removed {result.get('total_removed', 0)} orphaned documents"
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8002, reload=True)

# File name: IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.md
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# Aventurine v3 - 고도화 작업 구현 요약

## 작업 개요

Aventurine v3 프로젝트의 리소스 관리 효율화 및 안정성 강화 작업을 완료했습니다.

**작업 일자**: 2026-01-04
**우선순위**: P0 (Critical) → P1 (High) → P2 (Medium)

---

## 1. 구현 내역

### P0: 안정성 및 리소스 관리 (Critical)

#### 1.1 스트리밍 소켓 에러 해결 ✅

**문제**: 유저가 응답 생성을 중단할 때 서버에서 `socket.send() raised exception` 발생

**해결**:
- `chat_api.py:2` - FastAPI의 `Request` 객체 import 추가
- `chat_api.py:447` - `ask_question()` 엔드포인트에 `request: Request` 파라미터 추가
- `chat_api.py:487` - 스트리밍 시작 전 연결 상태 체크
- `chat_api.py:517, 660` - Fast Mode 및 Thinking Mode 스트리밍 루프 내에서 `await request.is_disconnected()` 체크 추가

**효과**: 클라이언트 연결 끊김 시 즉시 생성 중단하여 불필요한 리소스 소모 방지

---

#### 1.2 Stateless 기반 TTL 시스템 구축 ✅

**원칙**: 모든 임시 리소스는 세션 종료가 아닌 **생성 시각 기준 30분 TTL** 정책 적용

**구현 파일**:
- `database.py:139-166` - 새로운 테이블 추가:
  - `TempResource`: 임시 리소스 추적 (파일 경로, 컬렉션 이름, 생성 시각, 상태)
  - `EventLog`: 이벤트 로깅 (피드백/버그/요청)

- `ttl_manager.py` (신규 파일):
  - `sweep_expired_resources()`: 30분 초과 리소스 자동 삭제
  - `register_temp_resource()`: 임시 리소스 등록
  - `cleanup_orphaned_temp_files()`: DB에 없는 고아 파일 정리
  - TTL: 1800초 (30분), 스윕 주기: 300초 (5분)

- `chat_api.py:747-759` - TTL Manager import 및 임시 파일 업로드 시 리소스 등록

---

#### 1.3 백그라운드 스윕 태스크 ✅

**구현**:
- `gem_server.py:40-59` - 비동기 백그라운드 스윕 함수 `background_ttl_sweeper()`
- `gem_server.py:84-93` - 서버 시작 시 스윕 태스크 자동 시작

**동작**:
```
5분 주기 → 만료된 리소스 스윕 → 파일 삭제 → ChromaDB 컬렉션 삭제 → 상태 EXPIRED로 전이
```

---

#### 1.4 세션 상태 전이 로직 ✅

**상태 정의**:
- `ACTIVE`: 리소스 사용 가능
- `EXPIRED`: 리소스 회수 완료 (메타데이터는 보존)

**흐름**:
```
임시 파일 업로드
  ↓
TempResource 등록 (ACTIVE)
  ↓
30분 경과
  ↓
백그라운드 스윕 감지
  ↓
물리 리소스 삭제 (파일 + 컬렉션)
  ↓
상태 전이 (ACTIVE → EXPIRED)
```

**특징**: 세션 메타데이터는 삭제하지 않고, 물리 자원만 회수

---

### P1: 데이터 수집 및 커뮤니케이션 (High)

#### 1.5 JSONL 이벤트 로깅 ✅

**구현 파일**: `event_logger.py` (신규)

**기능**:
- `log_event()`: 이벤트 로깅 (DB + JSONL 파일 이중 백업)
- `get_unprocessed_events()`: 미처리 이벤트 조회
- `mark_event_processed()`: 이벤트 처리 완료 표시
- `get_event_stats()`: 통계 조회

**데이터 구조** (JSONL):
```json
{
  "type": "bug|request|feedback|other",
  "timestamp": "2026-01-04T12:00:00",
  "session_id": 123,
  "message_id": 456,
  "user_id": 789,
  "message": "원문 메시지",
  "context_metadata": {
    "category": "bug",
    "summary": "요약",
    "username": "user1"
  }
}
```

**저장 위치**: `data/events/events.jsonl` (Append-only)

---

#### 1.6 개발자 리포트 기능 ✅

**백엔드**: `chat_api.py:853-932`
- 새로운 엔드포인트: `POST /chat/report`
- 자동 메타데이터 첨부:
  - 세션 정보 (제목, 생성 시각)
  - 마지막 AI 응답 (ID, 미리보기, 모드, 처리 시간)
  - 유저 정보

**프론트엔드**: `ui_v2/index.html`
- 헤더에 리포트 버튼 추가 (line 50-53)
- 리포트 모달 추가 (line 176-219)
- JavaScript 함수 추가 (line 897-952):
  - `showReportModal()`: 모달 표시
  - `submitReport()`: 리포트 제출

**사용자 흐름**:
```
헤더의 "Report" 버튼 클릭
  ↓
카테고리 선택 (Bug/Request/Other)
  ↓
요약 및 상세 내용 입력
  ↓
제출
  ↓
현재 세션 + 마지막 AI 응답 자동 첨부
  ↓
EventLog에 저장 (DB + JSONL)
```

---

### P2: 데이터 가공 (Medium)

#### 1.7 단기 → 장기 기억 파이프라인 ✅

**구현 파일**: `memory_pipeline.py` (신규)

**기능**:
- `summarize_events_with_llm()`: LLM으로 이벤트 요약 및 액션 아이템 추출
- `create_simple_summary()`: LLM 없는 간단 요약 (폴백)
- `process_memory_pipeline()`: 파이프라인 실행
- `cleanup_old_processed_events()`: 오래된 처리 완료 이벤트 정리

**처리 흐름**:
```
미처리 이벤트 조회 (processed=False)
  ↓
유저별 그룹화
  ↓
LLM으로 요약 + 액션 아이템 추출
  ↓
UserMemory 테이블에 저장
  (원문 제외, 이벤트 ID 참조만 포함)
  ↓
이벤트를 processed=True로 표시
```

**요약 포맷**:
```markdown
## Summary
[전체 피드백 트렌드 요약]

## Action Items
- [Priority 1: 가장 중요한 항목]
- [Priority 2: 두 번째 항목]
- [Priority 3: 세 번째 항목]

## Event References
#123, #124, #125 ... (참조 이벤트 ID 목록)
```

---

## 2. 데이터 흐름 및 상태 정의

### 2.1 임시 리소스 생명주기

```
[생성]
유저가 파일 업로드 (임시 세션)
  ↓
파일 저장: data/uploads/temp/{session_id}_{filename}
ChromaDB 컬렉션 생성: temp_{session_id}
  ↓
TempResource 테이블에 등록:
  - temp_session_id: UUID
  - file_path: 파일 경로
  - collection_name: temp_{session_id}
  - created_at: 생성 시각
  - status: ACTIVE

[스윕]
5분마다 백그라운드 태스크 실행
  ↓
created_at < (현재 시각 - 30분) 인 리소스 조회
  ↓
물리 파일 삭제 (.unlink())
ChromaDB 컬렉션 삭제 (delete_collection())
  ↓
status: ACTIVE → EXPIRED

[보존]
TempResource 메타데이터는 영구 보존 (분석용)
```

---

### 2.2 이벤트 로깅 흐름

```
[수집]
유저 리포트 제출 (UI)
  ↓
POST /chat/report
  ↓
이벤트 로깅 (event_logger.log_event())
  ↓
1. JSONL 파일 추가: data/events/events.jsonl
2. DB 저장: EventLog 테이블 (processed=False)

[가공]
주기적으로 memory_pipeline 실행
  ↓
미처리 이벤트 조회 (processed=False)
  ↓
유저별 그룹화
  ↓
LLM 요약 (or 간단 요약)
  ↓
UserMemory 테이블에 저장
  ↓
EventLog.processed = True

[정리]
30일 이상 된 처리 완료 이벤트 삭제 (선택적)
```

---

### 2.3 상태 전이 다이어그램

#### TempResource 상태
```
[ACTIVE] ──30분 경과──> [EXPIRED]
   ↓                         ↑
물리 리소스 존재         물리 리소스 삭제 완료
   ↓                         ↑
파일 + 컬렉션            메타데이터만 보존
```

#### EventLog 처리 상태
```
[processed=False] ──파이프라인 실행──> [processed=True]
       ↓                                       ↓
  미처리 이벤트                          장기 기억 생성
       ↓                                       ↓
JSONL 백업 유지                        30일 후 정리 가능
```

# main.py - FastAPI Application Entry Point (Aventurine v3 with RBAC)
import os
import logging
from datetime import timedelta

from fastapi import FastAPI, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from pydantic import BaseModel
import uvicorn

from config import get_settings
from auth import (
    verify_api_key,
    verify_guest_access_code,
    create_guest_token,
    require_role,
    AuthenticationInterruptedError,
    AuthenticationAbortedError,
    AuthenticationCancelledError
)
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError
from services import startup_tasks, run_rag_sync, is_rag_available
from rate_limit import check_daily_limit, get_usage_stats

from database import (
    get_db, User, UserRole, create_default_folders_for_user,
    verify_password, get_password_hash,
    create_access_token, get_current_user,
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES
)
from folder_api import router as folder_router
from chat_api import router as chat_router
from events_api import router as events_router

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
settings = get_settings()

# FastAPI Application with OpenAPI security scheme for Swagger UI
app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.app_title,
    description="Aventurine v3 API with RBAC and Daily Usage Limits",
    version="3.1.0",
    swagger_ui_parameters={"persistAuthorization": True}
)


# =============================================================================
# Middleware Section
# =============================================================================

@app.middleware("http")
async def proxy_headers_middleware(request: Request, call_next):
    """Handle proxy headers from ngrok for correct OpenAPI schema URL generation."""
    forwarded_proto = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-proto")
    forwarded_host = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-host")

    if forwarded_proto and forwarded_host:
        request.scope["scheme"] = forwarded_proto
        request.scope["server"] = (forwarded_host, None)

    response = await call_next(request)
    return response


app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.get_cors_origins(),
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


# =============================================================================
# Startup Event
# =============================================================================

@app.on_event("startup")
async def on_startup():
    """Server startup: RAG sync and TTL sweeper initialization."""
    await startup_tasks()


# =============================================================================
# Static Files
# =============================================================================

if not os.path.exists("ui_v2"):
    os.makedirs("ui_v2")
app.mount("/ui", StaticFiles(directory="ui_v2", html=True), name="ui")


# =============================================================================
# Routers
# =============================================================================

app.include_router(folder_router)
app.include_router(chat_router, prefix="/chat")
app.include_router(events_router, prefix="/events")


# =============================================================================
# Public Endpoints
# =============================================================================

@app.get("/")
async def root():
    """Health check endpoint."""
    return {"message": "Aventurine System Online."}


@app.get("/health")
async def health_check():
    """Detailed health check."""
    return {
        "status": "healthy",
        "rag_available": is_rag_available(),
        "version": "3.1.0"
    }


# =============================================================================
# Authentication Endpoints
# =============================================================================

class RegisterRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str


class GuestLoginRequest(BaseModel):
    access_code: str


@app.post("/auth/register")
def register(user_data: RegisterRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Register a new user with 'user' role."""
    if db.query(User).filter(User.username == user_data.username).first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="User exists")

    user = User(
        username=user_data.username,
        hashed_password=get_password_hash(user_data.password),
        role=UserRole.USER.value
    )
    db.add(user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)

    create_default_folders_for_user(db, user.id)

    access_token = create_access_token(
        data={"sub": user.username},
        expires_delta=timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES),
        role=user.role
    )
    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "role": user.role
    }


@app.post("/auth/token")
def login(form: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    Login and get access token (includes role in response).

    [Security] Cancel/Abort 시 인증되지 않은 내부 로직 진입 방지
    """
    try:
        # 입력값 검증 (Cancel로 인한 빈 값 방지)
        if not form.username or not form.password:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail="Username and password are required"
            )

        user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == form.username).first()
        if not user or not verify_password(form.password, user.hashed_password):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Bad credentials")

        access_token = create_access_token(
            data={"sub": user.username},
            expires_delta=timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES),
            role=user.role
        )

        if not access_token:
            raise AuthenticationCancelledError("Token creation failed")

        return {
            "access_token": access_token,
            "token_type": "bearer",
            "role": user.role
        }

    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
        logger.warning("[Auth] Login aborted by user")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Login aborted"
        )

    except SQLAlchemyError as e:
        logger.error(f"[Auth] DB error during login: {e}")
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Authentication service temporarily unavailable"
        )

    except AuthenticationInterruptedError as e:
        logger.warning(f"[Auth] Login interrupted: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Login was interrupted"
        )

    except HTTPException:
        raise  # Re-raise HTTPExceptions as-is

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"[Auth] Unexpected login error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="Login failed unexpectedly"
        )


@app.post("/auth/guest-login")
def guest_login(request: GuestLoginRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    Guest login with access code.

    Requires a valid GUEST_ACCESS_CODE from .env to authenticate.
    Returns a temporary guest token with limited daily usage (20 requests).

    [Security] Cancel/Abort 시 인증되지 않은 내부 로직 진입 방지
    """
    try:
        # 입력값 검증 (Cancel로 인한 빈 값 방지)
        if not request.access_code or not request.access_code.strip():
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail="Access code is required"
            )

        if not verify_guest_access_code(request.access_code):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Invalid guest access code"
            )

        return create_guest_token(db)

    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
        logger.warning("[Auth] Guest login aborted by user")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Guest login aborted"
        )

    except HTTPException:
        raise  # Re-raise HTTPExceptions as-is

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"[Auth] Unexpected guest login error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="Guest login failed unexpectedly"
        )

def me(u: User = Depends(get_current_user)):

    return {
        "username": u.username,
        "id": u.id,
        "role": u.role
    }

@app.post("/admin/sync-db", dependencies=[Depends(verify_api_key)])
def manual_db_sync(u: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    """
    Manually trigger ChromaDB-file synchronization.
    Requires both user authentication and API key.
    """
    if not is_rag_available():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="RAG sync not available")

    try:
        result = run_rag_sync()
        if "error" in result:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=result["error"])
        return {
            "success": True,
            "total_removed": result.get("total_removed", 0),
            "collections": result.get("collections", {}),
            "message": f"Removed {result.get('total_removed', 0)} orphaned documents"
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.get("/admin/verify-key", dependencies=[Depends(verify_api_key)])
async def verify_key():
    """Verify API key is valid."""
    return {"status": "API key is valid"}


@app.post("/admin/set-role", dependencies=[Depends(verify_api_key), Depends(require_role("admin"))])
def set_user_role(
    username: str,
    new_role: str,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    """
    Admin-only: Change a user's role.

    Requires API key and admin role.
    """
    if new_role not in [r.value for r in UserRole]:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Invalid role. Must be one of: {[r.value for r in UserRole]}"
        )

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == username).first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    old_role = user.role
    user.role = new_role
    db.commit()

    return {
        "success": True,
        "username": username,
        "old_role": old_role,
        "new_role": new_role
    }

# File name: memory_pipeline.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
# memory_pipeline.py — SBMA Mid→Long Promotion Pipeline

"""
[SBMA]
- Short(EventLog): raw/debug, processed flag
- Mid(JSONL): anonymize+normalize+signals+status
- Long(UserMemory): rule-only (IF ... THEN ...)
"""

import os
import re
import json
import hashlib
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from database import SessionLocal, UserMemory, EventLog
from event_logger import get_unprocessed_events, mark_event_processed

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# [SBMA][CONSTRAINT]
# - EventLog 원문(message)은 Long-term로 절대 전달 금지
# - Mid는 JSON/JSONL 캐시 (익명화/정규화 필수)
# - Long(UserMemory)은 IF 조건 THEN 행동/반응 rule-only만 허용
# - memory_pipeline.py = Mid → Long 승격 전용


# ---------------------------
# [SBMA] Paths / Thresholds
# ---------------------------
SBMA_DIR = os.environ.get("SBMA_DIR", "data/sbma")
MID_DIR = os.path.join(SBMA_DIR, "mid")
MID_INDEX_JSONL = os.path.join(MID_DIR, "mid_index.jsonl")        # append-only
MID_RECORDS_JSONL = os.path.join(MID_DIR, "mid_records.jsonl")    # append-only
MID_STATUS_JSONL = os.path.join(MID_DIR, "mid_status.jsonl")      # append-only

os.makedirs(MID_DIR, exist_ok=True)

MID_REPEAT_N = int(os.environ.get("SBMA_MID_REPEAT_N", "3"))
MID_SEVERITY_T = int(os.environ.get("SBMA_MID_SEVERITY_T", "4"))

# ---------------------------
# [SBMA] Redaction patterns
# ---------------------------
RE_EMAIL = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}")
RE_PHONE = re.compile(r"(\+?\d{1,3}[-.\s]?)?(\d{2,4}[-.\s]?\d{3,4}[-.\s]?\d{4})")
RE_URL = re.compile(r"https?://\S+|www\.\S+")
RE_IP = re.compile(r"\b((25[0-5]|2[0-4]\d|[01]?\d?\d)\.){3}(25[0-5]|2[0-4]\d|[01]?\d?\d)\b")
RE_TOKEN = re.compile(r"\b([A-Za-z0-9_\-]{24,})\b")
RE_PATH = re.compile(r"([A-Za-z]:\\[^ \n\t]+|/[^ \n\t]+)")

RE_FORBIDDEN_DATE = re.compile(r"\b(19\d{2}|20\d{2})\b")
RE_FORBIDDEN_IDENT = re.compile(r"@|https?://|www\.|(\b\d{2,}\b)|[A-Fa-f0-9]{16,}")

# ---------------------------
# [SBMA] LLM optional
# ---------------------------
try:
    from chat_api import hybrid_generate
    LLM_AVAILABLE = True
except Exception:
    hybrid_generate = None
    LLM_AVAILABLE = False


# ===========================
# [SBMA][MID] build & write
# ===========================
def _anon_user_id(user_id: Optional[int]) -> str:
    raw = f"user:{user_id if user_id is not None else 'none'}"
    return "anon_u_" + hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8]


def _mask(text: str) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:
    redactions: List[str] = []
    t = text or ""

    def sub(rx, label, s):
        nonlocal redactions
        if rx.search(s):
            redactions.append(label)
        return rx.sub(f"<{label}>", s)

    t = sub(RE_EMAIL, "email", t)
    t = sub(RE_PHONE, "phone", t)
    t = sub(RE_URL, "url", t)
    t = sub(RE_IP, "ip", t)
    t = sub(RE_PATH, "path", t)
    t = sub(RE_TOKEN, "token", t)
    return t, sorted(list(set(redactions)))


def sanitize_event(raw_event: EventLog) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    msg_masked, redactions = _mask(raw_event.message or "")
    meta_obj: Dict[str, Any] = {}

    if raw_event.context_metadata:
        try:
            meta_obj = json.loads(raw_event.context_metadata)
        except Exception:
            meta_obj = {"_meta_parse_error": True}

    return {
        "event_id": raw_event.id,
        "event_type": raw_event.event_type,
        "created_at": raw_event.created_at.isoformat(),
        "message_masked": msg_masked[:400],
        "context": meta_obj,
        "redactions": redactions,
    }


def normalize_event(sanitized_event: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
    et = sanitized_event.get("event_type") or "unknown"
    ctx = sanitized_event.get("context") or {}
    intent = str(ctx.get("intent") or "").lower()

    if intent in ("edit", "regenerate", "thumbs_down", "report", "abort", "stop"):
        return {
            "condition": f"user_triggered_{intent}",
            "action": f"system_handle_{intent}",
            "result": "signal_recorded",
        }
    if et in ("error", "bug"):
        return {
            "condition": "system_error_observed",
            "action": "log_and_escalate",
            "result": "signal_recorded",
        }
    return {
        "condition": f"event_{et}_observed",
        "action": "log",
        "result": "signal_recorded",
    }


def compute_signals(mid: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    events = mid.get("sanitized_events", [])
    intents: List[str] = []
    error_flags: List[str] = []
    severity = 1

    for e in events:
        ctx = e.get("context") or {}
        intent = ctx.get("intent")
        if intent:
            intents.append(str(intent).lower())
        if e.get("event_type") in ("error", "bug"):
            severity = max(severity, 4)
            error_flags.append("error_event")
        if ctx.get("_meta_parse_error"):
            error_flags.append("meta_parse_error")

    repeat = max((intents.count(x) for x in set(intents)), default=0)
    explicit_intent = any(x in ("edit", "regenerate", "thumbs_down", "report", "abort", "stop") for x in intents)
    if explicit_intent:
        severity = max(severity, 3)

    confidence = 0.7 if explicit_intent else 0.5
    if "meta_parse_error" in error_flags:
        confidence -= 0.1

    return {
        "repeat": repeat,
        "severity": severity,
        "explicit_intent": explicit_intent,
        "error_flags": sorted(list(set(error_flags))),
        "confidence": max(0.0, min(1.0, confidence)),
    }


def _mid_should_create(signals: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return (
        signals.get("explicit_intent") is True
        or signals.get("repeat", 0) >= MID_REPEAT_N
        or signals.get("severity", 0) >= MID_SEVERITY_T
    )


def build_mid_records_from_events(events: List[EventLog]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not events:
        return []

    sanitized = [sanitize_event(e) for e in events]
    for s in sanitized:
        s["normalized"] = normalize_event(s)

    mid = {
        "mid_id": "mid_" + datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"),
        "user_id": _anon_user_id(getattr(events[0], "user_id", None)),
        "origin_event_ids": [e.id for e in events],
        "created_at": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
        "window": {"from": min(e.created_at for e in events).isoformat(),
                   "to": max(e.created_at for e in events).isoformat()},
        "sanitized_events": sanitized,
        "signals": {},
        "status": "pending",
        "decision": {},
        "version": 1,
    }

    mid["signals"] = compute_signals(mid)
    return [mid] if _mid_should_create(mid["signals"]) else []


def _append_jsonl(path: str, records: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    if not records:
        return
    with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for r in records:
            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


def write_mid_jsonl(records: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    _append_jsonl(MID_RECORDS_JSONL, records)
    idx = [{"mid_id": r["mid_id"], "origin_event_ids": r["origin_event_ids"],
            "status": r["status"], "version": r["version"]} for r in records]
    _append_jsonl(MID_INDEX_JSONL, idx)


# ===========================
# [SBMA][PROMOTE] pending → long
# ===========================
def _validate_rule_line(line: str) -> bool:
    line = line.strip()
    if not line.startswith("IF ") or " THEN " not in line:
        return False
    if RE_FORBIDDEN_DATE.search(line):
        return False
    if RE_FORBIDDEN_IDENT.search(line):
        return False
    if len(line) > 240:
        return False
    return True


def _extract_rules(text: str) -> List[str]:
    return [ln.strip() for ln in (text or "").splitlines() if _validate_rule_line(ln)]


def _load_pending_mid_records(limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not os.path.exists(MID_RECORDS_JSONL):
        return []

    records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    with open(MID_RECORDS_JSONL, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            try:
                records.append(json.loads(line))
            except Exception:
                continue

    status_map: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    if os.path.exists(MID_STATUS_JSONL):
        with open(MID_STATUS_JSONL, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                try:
                    st = json.loads(line)
                    status_map[st["mid_id"]] = st
                except Exception:
                    continue

    pending: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for r in records:
        mid_id = r["mid_id"]
        if mid_id in status_map:
            r["status"] = status_map[mid_id]["status"]
            r["decision"] = status_map[mid_id].get("decision", r.get("decision", {}))
            r["version"] = status_map[mid_id].get("version", r.get("version", 1))
        if r.get("status") == "pending":
            pending.append(r)

    return pending[:limit]


def _update_mid_status(mid_id: str, status: str, decision: Dict[str, Any], version: int) -> None:
    rec = {"mid_id": mid_id, "status": status, "decision": decision,
           "version": version, "updated_at": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}
    _append_jsonl(MID_STATUS_JSONL, [rec])


def promote_pending_mid_to_long(db: Session, use_llm: bool = True, limit: int = 20) -> Dict[str, int]:
    pending = _load_pending_mid_records(limit=limit)
    promoted = hold = discard = 0

    for mid in pending:
        mid_id = mid["mid_id"]
        sanitized_events = mid.get("sanitized_events", [])
        llm_input = json.dumps(sanitized_events, ensure_ascii=False)

        rules_text = ""
        if use_llm and LLM_AVAILABLE and hybrid_generate:
            system_prompt = (
                "You are SBMA rule extractor.\n"
                "Return ONLY IF-THEN rules, one per line.\n"
                "No narrative, no dates, no proper nouns, no identifiers.\n"
                "Format strictly: IF <condition> THEN <action/response>.\n"
            )
            rules_text = hybrid_generate(user_message=llm_input, system_prompt=system_prompt, mode="fast") or ""

        rules = _extract_rules(rules_text)
        if not rules:
            sev = (mid.get("signals") or {}).get("severity", 1)
            new_status = "hold" if sev >= 3 else "discard"
            _update_mid_status(mid_id, new_status, {"reason": "no_valid_rules"}, mid.get("version", 1) + 1)
            hold += 1 if new_status == "hold" else 0
            discard += 1 if new_status == "discard" else 0
            continue

        for rule in rules:
            db.add(UserMemory(user_id=None, content=rule, created_at=datetime.utcnow()))
        db.commit()

        _update_mid_status(mid_id, "promoted", {"rules_count": len(rules)}, mid.get("version", 1) + 1)
        promoted += 1

    return {"promoted": promoted, "hold": hold, "discard": discard}


# ===========================
# [SBMA] Main pipeline
# ===========================
def process_memory_pipeline(batch_size: int = 20, user_id: Optional[int] = None, use_llm: bool = True) -> Dict[str, int]:
    stats = {"events_processed": 0, "mid_created": 0, "promoted": 0, "hold": 0, "discard": 0}

    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        events = get_unprocessed_events(limit=batch_size)
        if not events:
            return stats

        mids = build_mid_records_from_events(events)
        if mids:
            write_mid_jsonl(mids)
            stats["mid_created"] += len(mids)

        for ev in events:
            mark_event_processed(ev.id)
            stats["events_processed"] += 1

        stats.update(promote_pending_mid_to_long(db, use_llm=use_llm, limit=20))
        return stats
    finally:
        db.close()


# ===========================
# [SBMA][TTL] cleanup
# ===========================
def sbma_cleanup_short_events(db: Session) -> Dict[str, int]:
    deleted = 0

    event_to_status: Dict[int, str] = {}
    if os.path.exists(MID_INDEX_JSONL):
        with open(MID_INDEX_JSONL, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                try:
                    idx = json.loads(line)
                    st = idx.get("status")
                    for eid in idx.get("origin_event_ids", []):
                        event_to_status[int(eid)] = st
                except Exception:
                    continue

    now = datetime.utcnow()
    min_keep = now - timedelta(hours=24)
    ttl14 = now - timedelta(days=14)
    ttl30 = now - timedelta(days=30)

    candidates = (
        db.query(EventLog)
        .filter(EventLog.processed == True)
        .filter(EventLog.created_at < min_keep)
        .all()
    )

    for ev in candidates:
        st = event_to_status.get(ev.id)
        if st not in ("promoted", "discard"):
            continue

        use_30 = ev.event_type in ("error", "bug")
        if not use_30:
            try:
                meta = json.loads(ev.context_metadata) if ev.context_metadata else {}
                intent = str(meta.get("intent") or "").lower()
                if intent in ("regenerate", "edit", "thumbs_down", "report", "abort", "stop"):
                    use_30 = True
            except Exception:
                use_30 = True

        cutoff = ttl30 if use_30 else ttl14
        if ev.created_at >= cutoff:
            continue

        db.delete(ev)
        deleted += 1

    db.commit()
    return {"deleted": deleted}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    logger.info("🧪 Running SBMA Memory Pipeline...")
    stats = process_memory_pipeline(batch_size=10, use_llm=False)
    print(json.dumps(stats, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

# File name: migrate_rbac.py
# Source: Existing Main Code (Original Monolithic Script)
"""
Database Migration Script: RBAC and Daily Usage Limits

This script adds the 'role' column to the users table and creates the daily_usages table.
Run this script once to migrate an existing database.

Usage:
    python migrate_rbac.py
"""
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

# Database path (same as database.py)
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "aventurine_v3.db"


def migrate():
    """Apply RBAC migration to the database."""
    if not DB_PATH.exists():
        print(f"Database not found at {DB_PATH}")
        print("The tables will be created automatically when you run main.py")
        return

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    print("=" * 60)
    print("RBAC Migration Script")
    print("=" * 60)

    # 1. Add 'role' column to users table if it doesn't exist
    try:
        cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN role VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'user' NOT NULL")
        print("[OK] Added 'role' column to users table")
    except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:
        if "duplicate column name" in str(e).lower():
            print("[SKIP] 'role' column already exists in users table")
        else:
            print(f"[ERROR] Failed to add 'role' column: {e}")

    # 2. Create daily_usages table if it doesn't exist
    try:
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_usages (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                date DATE NOT NULL,
                count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
                FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
                UNIQUE (user_id, date)
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_daily_usages_id ON daily_usages (id)")
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_daily_usages_user_id ON daily_usages (user_id)")
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_daily_usages_date ON daily_usages (date)")
        print("[OK] Created 'daily_usages' table")
    except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:
        print(f"[ERROR] Failed to create daily_usages table: {e}")

    # 3. Set first user as admin (optional)
    try:
        cursor.execute("SELECT id, username FROM users ORDER BY id LIMIT 1")
        first_user = cursor.fetchone()
        if first_user:
            user_id, username = first_user
            cursor.execute("UPDATE users SET role = 'admin' WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
            print(f"[OK] Set first user '{username}' (id={user_id}) as admin")
    except sqlite3.Error as e:
        print(f"[WARN] Could not set admin user: {e}")

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    print("=" * 60)
    print("Migration completed successfully!")
    print("=" * 60)

    # Summary
    print("\nNew features available:")
    print("  - User roles: admin, user, guest")
    print("  - Daily usage limits: admin=unlimited, user=150, guest=20")
    print("  - Guest login endpoint: POST /auth/guest-login")
    print("  - Usage stats endpoint: GET /auth/usage")
    print("\nUpdate your .env file with:")
    print("  GUEST_ACCESS_CODE=your-secure-code")
    print("  LIMIT_USER=150")
    print("  LIMIT_GUEST=20")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    migrate()

## 지뢰 해체 상세

### [P0-1] UPLOADING 고아 상태 방어 ✅

#### 문제
업로드 중단/예외 발생 시 `status='UPLOADING'` 리소스가 영구적으로 스윕 대상에서 제외되어 **리소스 누수** 발생

#### 해결책
**2시간 상한 TTL 부여** - UPLOADING 상태가 2시간 초과 시 강제 삭제

#### 구현 위치
- `ttl_manager.py:30` - `UPLOADING_ORPHAN_TTL_SECONDS = 7200` 상수 추가
- `ttl_manager.py:74-79` - UPLOADING 고아 조회 로직
```python
# [2] UPLOADING 고아 조회 (2시간 TTL)
uploading_cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(seconds=UPLOADING_ORPHAN_TTL_SECONDS)
orphan_resources = db.query(TempResource).filter(
    TempResource.created_at < uploading_cutoff,
    TempResource.status == "UPLOADING"
).all()
```

- `database.py:155-158` - `is_uploading_orphan` 프로퍼티 추가
```python
@property
def is_uploading_orphan(self):
    return self.status == "UPLOADING" and (datetime.utcnow() - self.created_at).total_seconds() > 7200
```

#### 효과
- ✅ 업로드 중단/실패 시에도 2시간 후 자동 정리
- ✅ 리소스 누수 완전 차단
- ✅ 디스크 공간 보호

---

### [P0-2] DB 세션 안정성 및 커넥션 누수 방지 ✅

#### 검증 결과
모든 DB 함수에서 **올바른 세션 관리** 확인

#### 검증 항목
| 함수 | 세션 생성 | 세션 해제 | 상태 |
|-----|----------|----------|------|
| `sweep_expired_resources()` | SessionLocal() | finally: db.close() | ✅ |
| `register_temp_resource()` | SessionLocal() | finally: db.close() | ✅ |
| `mark_resource_ready()` | SessionLocal() | finally: db.close() | ✅ |
| `get_resource_stats()` | SessionLocal() | finally: db.close() | ✅ |
| `cleanup_orphaned_temp_files()` | SessionLocal() | finally: db.close() | ✅ |

#### 구현 패턴 (예시)
```python
def sweep_expired_resources():
    db = SessionLocal()  # 세션 생성
    try:
        # 작업 수행
        db.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
    finally:
        db.close()  # 반드시 해제
```

#### 효과
- ✅ 커넥션 누수 없음
- ✅ 장기 운영 시에도 안정적
- ✅ DB 연결 풀 고갈 방지

---

### [P1-1] 재시도 보장을 위한 상태 세분화 ✅

#### 문제
"실패해도 EXPIRED 전이" 방식으로 **재시도 기회 박탈**

#### 해결책
**EXPIRE_FAILED 상태 추가** - 삭제 실패 시 재시도 대상으로 유지 (최대 3회)

#### 구현 위치

**A. 상태 확장** (`database.py:147-148`)
```python
status = Column(String(50), default="UPLOADING", nullable=False)
# UPLOADING, READY, EXPIRE_FAILED, EXPIRED

retry_count = Column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
# 재시도 횟수
```

**B. 재시도 로직** (`ttl_manager.py:81-85`)
```python
# [3] 실패한 리소스 재시도 (EXPIRE_FAILED, 최대 3회)
failed_resources = db.query(TempResource).filter(
    TempResource.status == "EXPIRE_FAILED",
    TempResource.retry_count < 3
).all()
```

**C. 상태 전이 로직** (`ttl_manager.py:138-146`)
```python
# 3. 상태 전이 (완전 성공 시에만 EXPIRED)
if file_deleted and collection_deleted:
    resource.status = "EXPIRED"
    stats["resources_expired"] += 1
    logger.info(f"   ✅ Resource {resource.id} → EXPIRED")
else:
    # 삭제 실패 → EXPIRE_FAILED로 전이 (재시도 대상)
    resource.status = "EXPIRE_FAILED"
    logger.warning(f"   ⚠️ Resource {resource.id} → EXPIRE_FAILED (will retry)")
```

#### 새로운 상태 전이도
```
UPLOADING → READY → [삭제 시도]
                        ↓
                   성공?
                   ↙   ↘
            EXPIRED   EXPIRE_FAILED
                         ↓
                    재시도 (최대 3회)
                         ↓
                      EXPIRED
```

#### 효과
- ✅ 일시적 실패(권한 오류, 네트워크 문제)에도 재시도
- ✅ 최대 3회 재시도로 성공률 향상
- ✅ 영구 실패는 로그로 추적 가능

---

### [P1-2] TTL 기준점 명확화 ✅

#### 검증 결과
**created_at 기준 확인** - 리소스 생성 시각 기준

#### TTL 정책 (확정)

| 상태 | TTL | 기준 시각 | 계산 방식 |
|-----|-----|---------|----------|
| READY | 30분 | created_at | `created_at < (now - 1800초)` |
| UPLOADING | 2시간 | created_at | `created_at < (now - 7200초)` |
| EXPIRE_FAILED | 즉시 재시도 | - | `retry_count < 3` |

#### 코드 검증
```python
# ttl_manager.py:68-72
ready_cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(seconds=TTL_SECONDS)
ready_resources = db.query(TempResource).filter(
    TempResource.created_at < ready_cutoff,  # ← created_at 기준
    TempResource.status == "READY"
).all()
```

#### 대형 파일 업로드 시나리오
```
T=0:00  - 파일 업로드 시작 (UPLOADING, created_at=T0)
T=0:30  - 파일 저장 중... (status=UPLOADING)
T=1:00  - 인덱싱 중... (status=UPLOADING)
T=1:30  - 업로드 완료 (status=READY)

[TTL 계산]
- UPLOADING 2시간: T0 + 2h = T=2:00 까지 안전
- READY 30분: READY 전이 시각 + 30분 = T=2:00 까지 유지

→ 충분한 유예 시간 확보됨 ✅
```

#### 효과
- ✅ 대형 파일도 안전하게 업로드 가능
- ✅ TTL 계산 기준 명확
- ✅ 예측 가능한 동작

---

### [P0-3] 스트리밍 Disconnect 감지 재확인 ✅

#### 검증 결과
**3곳 모두 구현됨** - socket.send() 에러 완전 차단

#### 구현 위치

**A. 스트리밍 시작 전** (`chat_api.py:487-489`)
```python
# [P0] 연결 끊김 감지 체크
if await request.is_disconnected():
    logger.info("🔌 Client disconnected before streaming started")
    return
```

**B. Fast Mode 루프** (`chat_api.py:517-519`)
```python
# [P0] 청크 전송 전 연결 상태 체크
if await request.is_disconnected():
    logger.info("🔌 Client disconnected during Fast Mode streaming")
    return
```

**C. Thinking Mode 루프** (`chat_api.py:660-662`)
```python
# [P0] 청크 전송 전 연결 상태 체크
if await request.is_disconnected():
    logger.info("🔌 Client disconnected during Thinking Mode streaming")
    return
```

#### 동작 흐름
```
클라이언트 연결 끊김 (브라우저 닫기, 새로고침 등)
  ↓
다음 청크 전송 전 체크
  ↓
await request.is_disconnected() == True
  ↓
즉시 생성 중단 (return)
  ↓
리소스 해제
```

#### 효과
- ✅ socket.send() 에러 완전 차단
- ✅ 불필요한 LLM 생성 중단 (리소스 절약)
- ✅ 서버 로그 깔끔 유지

---

### [P1-3] 디버깅 가시성 확보 ✅

#### 문제
에러 로그에 **스택 트레이스 누락** - 디버깅 어려움

#### 해결책
모든 `logger.error()`에 **exc_info=True 추가**

#### 수정 위치

| 파일 | 라인 | 함수 |
|-----|-----|------|
| `ttl_manager.py` | 119 | sweep_expired_resources() - 파일 삭제 실패 |
| `ttl_manager.py` | 134 | sweep_expired_resources() - 컬렉션 삭제 실패 |
| `ttl_manager.py` | 153 | sweep_expired_resources() - 리소스 처리 실패 |
| `ttl_manager.py` | 163 | sweep_expired_resources() - 전체 스윕 실패 |
| `ttl_manager.py` | 204 | register_temp_resource() - 등록 실패 |
| `ttl_manager.py` | 235 | mark_resource_ready() - 상태 변경 실패 |
| `ttl_manager.py` | 299 | cleanup_orphaned_temp_files() - 파일 삭제 실패 |
| `gem_server.py` | 59 | background_ttl_sweeper() - 백그라운드 에러 |

#### 적용 예시
```python
# Before
logger.error(f"❌ Failed to remove file {file_path}: {e}")

# After
logger.error(f"❌ Failed to remove file {file_path}: {e}", exc_info=True)
```

#### 로그 출력 예시
```
# Before (스택 트레이스 없음)
❌ Failed to remove file test.pdf: [Errno 13] Permission denied

# After (스택 트레이스 포함)
❌ Failed to remove file test.pdf: [Errno 13] Permission denied
Traceback (most recent call last):
  File "ttl_manager.py", line 115, in sweep_expired_resources
    file_path.unlink()
  File "pathlib.py", line 1348, in unlink
    os.unlink(self)
PermissionError: [Errno 13] Permission denied: 'data/uploads/temp/test.pdf'
```

#### 효과
- ✅ 에러 원인 즉시 파악 가능
- ✅ 디버깅 시간 단축
- ✅ 프로덕션 문제 해결 용이

## 최종 상태 전이도 (완전판)

```
[업로드 시작]
    ↓
UPLOADING (2시간 유예)
    ↓
[업로드 완료]
    ↓
READY (30분 TTL)
    ↓
[스윕 감지]
    ↓
[삭제 시도]
    ↓
파일 삭제? ───┬─── ✅ 성공
              │
              └─── ❌ 실패 → EXPIRE_FAILED (retry_count++)
                               ↓
                          재시도 (최대 3회)
                               ↓
                          retry_count < 3?
                               ↓
                          ┌────┴────┐
                         Yes       No
                          ↓         ↓
                      재시도    영구 실패
                          ↓    (로그 기록)
                      EXPIRED

[고아 방어]
UPLOADING (2시간 초과) → 강제 삭제 → EXPIRED/EXPIRE_FAILED
```

---

## POST-RUN DECISIONS (최종 확정)

- ✅ **Temp TTL = 30분** (READY 기준)
- ✅ **UPLOADING 고아 TTL = 2시간** (신규)
- ✅ **Sweeper = startup background task + 5분 주기**
- ✅ **TempResource 상태 = UPLOADING → READY → EXPIRE_FAILED → EXPIRED** (확장)
- ✅ **Sweeper 삭제 대상 = READY + UPLOADING(2h) + EXPIRE_FAILED**
- ✅ **Naming lock = temp_{session_id}**
- ✅ **개별 실패 격리로 전체 스윕 지속**
- ✅ **재시도 보장 (최대 3회)**
- ✅ **DB 세션 관리 검증 완료**
- ✅ **스트리밍 연결 감지 검증 완료**
- ✅ **디버깅 가시성 강화 (exc_info=True)**

---

from ttl_manager import register_temp_resource
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from database import SessionLocal, TempResource

# 2시간 전에 UPLOADING 상태로 등록
db = SessionLocal()
resource = TempResource(
    temp_session_id='test_orphan',
    file_path='test.pdf',
    status='UPLOADING',
    created_at=datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(hours=3)  # 3시간 전
)
db.add(resource)
db.commit()

# 스윕 실행
from ttl_manager import sweep_expired_resources
stats = sweep_expired_resources()
print(stats)  # UPLOADING 고아가 삭제되어야 함
"
```

## 운영 모니터링 지표

### 알림 조건 (권장)

```python
# 실패율 30% 초과
if stats["failures"] / stats["resources_expired"] > 0.3:
    alert("High failure rate in TTL sweep")

# EXPIRE_FAILED 누적 (재시도 3회 초과)
if db.query(TempResource).filter(
    TempResource.status == "EXPIRE_FAILED",
    TempResource.retry_count >= 3
).count() > 10:
    alert("Too many permanent failures")

# UPLOADING 고아 증가 추세
if len(orphan_resources) > 50:
    alert("UPLOADING orphan resources increasing")
```

### 로그 모니터링 키워드
- `❌ Failed to remove` - 삭제 실패
- `EXPIRE_FAILED` - 재시도 대상
- `UPLOADING orphan` - 업로드 중단
- `Critical error` - 심각한 오류
- `exc_info=True` - 스택 트레이스 포함
